te.po.mī.yiṉ tiripārāycci
tamiḻilakkaṇattil ayaṉmoḻi yamaippukaḷ (Foreign Models in Tamil Grammar)
iṟaivaṉaruḷāl iyaṉṟa arainūṟṟāṇṭārāycciyāl, tamiḻ vērccoṟporuḷ perumpālum kāṇappeṟṟu, tamiḻ tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻi yeṉṉum uṇmai veḷḷiṭaimalaiyāy viḷaṅkiyapiṉ, atai uyirmutal vērccoṟkaḷ, uyirmeymmutal vērccoṟkaḷ eṉṉum iru vēṟu kaṭṭurait toṭarkaḷāl oruvāṟu kāṭṭi, iṟutiyil, tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai  nāṭṭum taṉiccoṟkaḷ eṉṟum tokutic coṟkaḷ eṉṟum iruvakaik kaṭṭurait toṭarkaḷāl nāṭṭi varuṅkāl, tamiḻaik kāṭṭik koṭukkum vaiyāpurikaḷiṉ vēlai mīṇṭum valuttu aracaiyum palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷaiyum mayakki, tamiḻ muṉṉēṟṟam cāṇēṟiyapiṉ muḻañcaṟukkiyiruppataṉāl, vīṭu oru puṟattil tīppaṟṟi vēkumpōtu atai yaṇaikkum varai vēṟoru puṟattil putitāy vēytalai niṟutti vaittal pōṉṟu, eṉ moḻiyārāyccik kaṭṭurait toṭar veḷi yīṭṭaik kuṟuṅkālikamāka niṟuttiviṭṭu, par. (Dr.) te.po.mī. yiṉ tamiḻilakkaṇattil ayaṉmoḻi yamaippukaḷ eṉṉum tīya nūl tīntamiḻkku mēlun tīṅku viḷaittalait taṭuttaṟporuṭṭu, ataic ciṟuciṟu pakutiyākap pakuttuc cīriya varalāṟṟu moḻinūṉ muṟaiyiṟ cekuttu, taviṭupoṭiyāyt takartteṟitalaik kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭatu it toṭar kaṭṭurai yeṉṟu terintukoḷka.
naṉṉāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattu,
palkuḻuvum pāḻceyyum uṭpakaiyum vēntalaikkum
kolkuṟumpum illatu nāṭu		(kuṟaḷ, 735)
eṉṟār teṉṉavar talaivar tiruvaḷḷuvar. avar kuṟitta mukkēṭukaḷum muṟṟi mutirntatu muttamiḻ nāṭu.
palkuḻu
mutaṟkaṇ piṟanāṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē piṟappoṭu toṭarpillātu iyaṅki vanta antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉṉum nālvakait talaimait toḻil vakuppukaḷum, vēḷāḷariṉ patiṉeṇ pakkat toḻil vakuppukaḷum, piṟkālattil āriyac cūḻcciyāl piṟappoṭu toṭarpu paṭuttappaṭṭu, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa akamaṇak kulaṅkaḷākap palkik kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkum pūḷaip pañcupōṟ paṟakkaṭikkappaṭṭuc ciṟitum oṟṟumaiyiṉṟic ciṉṉapiṉṉamākac citaiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa. atōṭu kalviyurimaiyiḻantu, mutaṟkālattil nūṟṟiṟkirupattaivar pāvalarāyirunta tamiḻ iṉam nūṟṟiṟkut toṇṇūṟṟuvar taṟkuṟikaḷāṉa tokutiyākavum māṟiṟṟu.
piṟavikkulap piriviṉaikkumēl āriya mata vēṟupāṭṭālum tamiḻar oṟṟumai kulaintatu. iṉṟu araciyaṟ kaṭcikaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa oṟṟumaikkēṭu koñcanañca maṉṟu.
in nilaiyil, tamiḻaik kāttaṟkum vaḷarttaṟkumeṉṟu tōṉṟiya kaḻakaṅkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭavaiyalla. maturait tamiḻccaṅkam, karantait tamiḻccaṅkam, tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiyat tamiḻccaṅkam, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam, tamiḻakap pulavar kuḻu,  ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉam, peṅkaḷūrt tamiḻccaṅkam  mutaliya palvēṟu kaḻakaṅkaḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷum kuḻukkaḷum, paḻaiya pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻk kaḻakampōl orē amaippaka māka oṉṟiṉālaṉṟit tamiḻukkup perunaṉmai ceytal iyalātu.
uṭpakai
paḻaṅkālap pētai mūvēntarum, āriyap pūcāriyariṉ veṇ ṇiṟattāl avarai nilattēva reṉṟum, eṭuppoliyāl avaratu ilakkiya moḻiyait tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum, muṟṟum nampiviṭṭataṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyai vaḻipāṭṭu  moḻiyāka uyarttavum tamiḻait tāḻttavum nērntuviṭṭatu. ak kāla vēntar kaṇkaṇṭa teyvamātalālum, aracaṉ eppaṭi, kuṭikaḷ appaṭi eṉpataṉālum, pulavar palarum avaraip piṉpaṟṟiviṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam aracarum kuṭikaḷum āriya vaḻippaṭṭataṟku, āriyap pūcāriyar civaṉiyam māliyam eṉṉum iru tamiḻ mataṅkaḷaiyum taḻuvi avaṟṟait tamaveṉṟu  ēmāṟṟiyatē aṭippaṭaik karaṇiyam.
āriyap pūcāriyariṉ vaḻiyiṉar pala araciyaṟ patavikaḷ ēṟṟataiyum, pāṭṭum kūttum payiṉṟataiyum, pala toḻilāḷaraiyum piṟappoṭu  toṭarpupaṭuttiyataiyum, kalviyait tamakkē urimaiyākka muyaṉṟa taiyum, tamiḻarait tāḻttit tammai uyartti vantataiyum, nuṇmāṇ nuḻaimatit teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvar nōkki, āriyac cūḻcci yeṉṟu kaṇṭa piṉṉarē, tamiḻarkku viḻippuṇarcciyūṭṭat tirukkuṟaḷai iyaṟṟiṉār.
akkālattil ulaka varalāṟṟaṟivum moḻinūlārāycciyum iṉmaiyāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar moḻittuṟaiyil āriyattai etirkka villai. āyiṉ, avar nūlāl tamiḻāriya vēṟupāṭṭuṇarcci tōṉṟiviṭṭataṉālēyē, 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaiyāpurip piḷḷai pōṉṟa koṇṭāṉ allatu kōṭaṉ eṉṉum kuyavaṉ, āriyam naṉṟu, tamiḻtītu eṉavuraittu, paritimāṟ kalaiñaṉ pōṉṟa nakkīrarāl cāvikkavum piṉṉar uyirppikkavum paṭṭāṉ.
11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil puttamittiraṉ mataveṟiyālum āriyattiṟ kaṭimaiyālum,18ām nūṟṟaṇṭil cuvāmināta tēcikar āriyattiṟ kaṭimaiyālum tamiḻaik kāṭṭik koṭukka nērntatu.
iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ulaka varalāṟṟaṟivum oppiyal moḻi nūlārāycciyum āṅkilak kalviyum pēccurimai, yeḻut turimaiyum mikuntiruppatāl, tamiḻaik kāṭṭikkoṭukkak kāraṇamē yillai. āyiṉum, āṅkilattiṟ paṭṭak kalviyum caṭṭak kalviyum peṟṟum, vēṟem moḻiyilu millāta poruḷilakkaṇaṅ koṇṭa tami ḻilakkiyaṅ kaṟṟum, vaiyāpurip piḷḷaiyum par. te. po. mī.yum tamiḻai vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatākak kāṭṭiyataṟku,
nuṇṇiya nūlpala kaṟpiṉum maṟṟuntaṉ
uṇmai yaṟivē mikum			(kuṟaḷ. 373)
eṉpatē kāraṇamātal kūṭum.
pārttamaṭṭil tōṉṟā allatu teḷivāyt tōṉṟā eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum viḻippat tōṉṟā eṉṉum eḷiya tolkāppiyat toṭarukku, tōṉṟavē tōṉṟā (beyond ascertainment)  eṉṟu vaiyāpurip piḷḷai kūṟiyatum,
makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa		(moḻi. 49)
eṉṉum pōlipaṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpāvai orumai, paṉmai eṇ paṟṟiyateṉṟu par. te.po.mī. kūṟiyatum, mēṟkuṟitta kuṟaḷālēyē viḷaṅkum.
ēṟattāḻa mūvāyirameṉṟu, oruvakaiyiṟ kaṇikkappaṭum ulaka moḻikaḷukkuḷ, tamiḻukkup pōṉṟu uṭpakai vēṟetaṟkum iruppatākat teriyavillai.
kolkuṟumpu
toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻariṭai vatintu, tamiḻariṭamiruntē ūṇuṭai yuṟaiyuḷ peṟṟu, tamiḻaiyē tāymoḻiyākavum vāḻ moḻiyākavuṅ koṇṭu, tamiḻarālēyē muṉṉēṟi, tamiḻar tuṇai yālēyē vāḻntu varum tamiḻnāṭṭup pirāmaṇar, ceynnaṉṟi  koṉṟu vaḷartta kaṭā mārpiṟ pāyntāṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻarai yetirttup piṟappiliḻintavareṉṟu tāḻtti, aṟivārāycci mikka iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum tamiḻai muṉpōl vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyākātavāṟu taṭuttu, ataṉ tūymaiyai iyaṉṟavarai keṭuttu, tamiḻk kalaiyaṟiviyal mūla nūlkaḷaiyum vaṭamoḻi moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉamākak kāṭṭivarukiṉṟaṉar.
itaṟkumēl, vaṭanāṭṭu intiyārum iṉṉoru kolkuṟumpākat  tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyum intiyum oṉṟukkoṉṟu tuṇai ceykiṉṟaṉa. iv viraṇṭaiyum ēṟkum pērāyat tamiḻar uṭpakaiyāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 
par. te.po.mī.
ivar vaiyāpurip piḷḷaiyai aṭiyoṟṟic celpavar; māntaṉ tōṉṟiyakattaiyum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakattaiyum āriyam tōṉṟiya vakaiyaiyum ārāyntaṟiyātavar; tamiḻiṉ kumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattai oppukkoḷḷātavar. tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷi ṉiṉṟu vantava reṉṟu vāyiṉāṟ collāviṭiṉum uḷḷattil oppuk koḷpavar;  tamiḻ vērccoṟkaḷai aṟiyātavar; ataṉāl āriyattai mutaṉmaiyākavum tamiḻai vaḻinilaiyākavum vaittu moḻiyilakkiya varalāṟukaḷ eḻuti varupavar (History of the Tamil Language, A History of Tamil Literature), ivar tamiḻk kalaik kaḷañciyattil cila kaṭṭuraikaḷum varaintuḷḷār. aṭippaṭai tavaṟāṉapaṭiyāl muṭipukaḷum perumpālum tavaṟākavē yirukkum. ivar iṟutiyāka eḻuti 1974-l veḷiyiṭṭa āṅkila nūl , tamiḻilakkaṇattil ayaṉmoḻiyamaippukaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ‘Foreign Models in Tamil Grammar’ eṉṉum talaippukkoṇṭatu. ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē (nūṉmarapu, 16) eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā ivar muṭipiṟkup perun tuṇai ceyvatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇam muḻuvataiyum ōraḷavu virivākak kūṟum orē paṇṭai nūlāyiṉum, iṭaiyiṭaip pala paṭukuḻi kaḷaiyum kavarvaḻikaḷaiyum koṇṭiruppatāl, tamiḻ vērc col lārāycciyum tamiḻaṉ piṟantaka ārāycciyum illātār ettuṇait teṉmoḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyum kaṟṟiruppiṉum, tolkāppiyattai moḻi varalāṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭutta iyalātu.
in nūlai ivaraikkoṇṭu eḻutuvittatu, kēraḷap palkalaik kaḻakattaic cērnta V.I. cuppiramaṇiyam, kaṉṉaṭap palkalaikkaḻakattaic cērnta R.C. irēmattu (Hiremath), tiruppatip palkalaikkaḻakattaic cērnta K. makātēva cāttiri (Sastri) ākiya mūvar kuḻu. im mūvaruḷ mutalvar tamiḻait talaikīḻākap paṭittavar; ēṉaiyiruvar tamiḻaṟiyāta perumāḷar.
in nūl, puttamittiraṉ iyaṟṟiya vīracōḻiyam, cuppiramaṇiya tīṭcitar iyaṟṟiya pirayōka vivēkam, cuvāmināta tēcikar iyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇak kottu ākiya mūṉṟu taḷḷupaṭi nūlkaḷaiyum viḻumiya aḷavai nūlākak koṇṭu, uṇmaikku māṟāṉavum tamiḻukkuk kēṭāṉavumāṉa muṭipukaḷaik kūṟuvatu.
tamiḻilakkaṇam vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyateṉṟu tirittuk kūṟuvatāl, nallicaip pulavarālum aṭippaṭṭa cāṉṟōrālum uṇmait tamiḻa rālum uyariya ilakkaṇa nūlārālum utavāk kaṭaiyeṉṟu otukkappaṭṭa kaḻicaṭai nūlkaḷaiyē, in nūlāciriyar karuvi nūlkaḷākat tēṭi yeṭuttuk koṇṭataṉāl, ivaratu maṉappāṉmai evarukkun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.
ivaratu it tiruppaṇikku 40,000 (nāṟpatiṉāyiram) urupā utaviyatu ti.mu.ka. aracē. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻaṉē tamiḻaṉiṭam peṟṟa tamiḻp paṇattaik koṇṭu tamiḻnilattilēyē tamiḻukkuk kēṭāṉa oru pōlip puṉṉūlai yuruvākkit tamiḻp pulavar muṉpē ulakiṭaip parappiyiruppatai, uḷḷuntoṟum uḷḷañ cuṭum.
camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭaiyil tamiḻaik kaṟṟut tamiḻaip piṟaḻa vuṇarnta ivarai, aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakam oru tamiḻatikāriyāka amarttiṟṟu. ataṉāl tamiḻukku viḷainta tīṅku koñcanañca maṉṟu.
mutalāvatu, ap palkalaikkaḻakattil eṉṉāl tokukkappaṭa virunta centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalip paṇi taṭuppuṇṭatu. ataṉāl veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭēṉ. eṉṉoṭu tamiḻum veḷiyēṟiṟṟu. av vakaramutaliyaip par. te.po.mī.yum tokuttārallar. avarāl tokukkavum iyalātu.
iraṇṭāvatu, kālṭuvelār kālamvarai kaiyāḷappaṭṭu vanta koṭivaḻiyiyal moḻinūl vilakkappaṭṭu, aṟiviyalallāta amerikka vaṇṇaṉai  moḻinūl pukuttappaṭṭatu. koṭivaḻiyiyal moḻinūlāl āriya ēmāṟṟuk kuṭṭu veḷiyākiviṭum. ātalāṟ pirāmaṇar atai virumpuvatillai. ataṉāl, avarvaḻic cellum te.po.mī.yum ataip puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭār.
mūṉṟāvatu kiḷaimoḻiyiyal eṉṉum peyarāl, centamiḻ marapai mīṟik koṭuntamiḻiṉuṅ kēṭāṉa kaṭuṅ koccaic coṟkaḷum uyarntōr vaḻakkiṟ koppākat tokukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  itu, kīḻōr paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum mēlōr mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatu pōlākiṉṟatu.
nāṉkāvatu, ulaka varalāṟu (World History), oppiyaṉ moḻinūl (Comparative Philology), kumukāyap paṇpāṭṭu māntaṉūl (Social and Cultural Anthropology) eṉṉum ulakantaḻīiya mūvaṟiviyalkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci kuṉṟukiṉṟatu.
aintāvatu, āriyam eṉṉum ilakkiyap pirāmaṇiyam valuttu varukiṉṟatu. itaṉ viḷaivāka, aṇmaiyil, patumā cuppiramaṇiyam eṉṉum pirāmaṇa naṭikaiyār, tamiḻarukku naṭakkalaiyē teriyāteṉṟum, kācumīrap parata muṉivariṉ parata cāttirattaik kaṟṟē ak kalaiyai aṟintaṉareṉṟum, or iṭunūl iṭṭu aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakat tiṟ paṇṭārakarp paṭṭam (Doctroate) peṟṟatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu, mīṉkuñcē tāymīṉai nīntap payiṟṟiyatu eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟai yottatu. ip paṭṭavaḷippu, aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻaka naṭappērāciriyarai maṭṭu maṉṟit tamiḻp pērāciriyaraiyum āṭcik kuḻuvaiyum talaimai yatikāri kaḷaiyum tākkukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl, muttamiḻ eṉṉum  peyarē muḻup poruḷaiyum iḻakkiṉṟatu. aracavayavar aṇṇāmalaic ceṭṭi yār avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻicaip pōrāṭṭam muṟṟum payaṉaṟṟatākiṉṟatu. aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakam  tamiḻ vaḷarccikkut tōṉṟiya teṉpa tum tavaṟākiṉṟatu. ulakat tamiḻp pulavar aṉaivarum aṭimaṭaiyarum aḵṟiṇaiyum ākiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cem moḻi yeṉṟum, ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa talaik kaḻakatti lēyē muttamiḻ tōṉṟiviṭṭa teṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ 4ām maṭittiripāṉa mēlai yāriyattiṉ kirēkkap pirivai yotta oru moḻiyaip pēcikkoṇṭu ilakkiyamum eḻuttumiṉṟi mullai nākarika nilaiyil ki.mu. 1500 pōl intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu tam moḻi vaḻak kiḻantu, vētamoḻiyaiyum ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa camaṟkiruta moḻiyaiyum ilakkiya moḻiyākavē koṇṭu, intiya moḻikaḷaiyē pēcivarum āriya vaḻiyiṉar ellā vakaiyilum tamiḻarālēyē nākarikamaṭaintu cūḻcci yākat tammai yuyarttik koṇṭu, tamiḻ mūlanūlkaḷai yellām vaṭamoḻiyil moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭapiṉ aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatai, aṟintu ammaiyārkkuk koṭutta paṭṭattai aṇṇā malaip palkalaikkaḻakattai mīṭṭuk koḷḷum kālam aṇmaiyi lirukkiṉṟatu eṉpatai, iṉṟu kutittuk kūttāṭik kulavik koṇṭāṭik koṭṭamaṭikkum tamiḻp pakaivar aṟivārāka.
kuṟippu: taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil par.te.po.mī. yoṭu eṉakku eṭṭuṇaiyum pakaimaiyillai.  avarai eṉ uṇmai naṇpar eṉavum piṉvāṅkēṉ.  āyiṉ, avarukkum eṉakkum potuvāṉa tāyaip paḻippatum muṉṉōrait takavilar eṉpatum pōṉṟa koḷkaiyaiyum kūṟṟaiyumē vaṉmaiyāy etirkkiṉṟēṉ.
mūvāyiravāṇṭu maṟaiyuṇṭu kiṭanta muttamiḻ māṇpu mīṇṭum ulakukku veḷippaṭavirukkiṉṟatu. itu iṟaivaṉ ēṟpāṭu. itai evarun taṭukka viyalātu. ātalāl, vaiyāpurip piḷḷaipōl vacaipeṟa vāḻātu, iṉimēlāyiṉum tirunti uṇmai viḷampiyeṉṟu   peya reṭukka.
mēlaiyaṟiñar iṟṟai aṟiviyalkaḷukkaṉṟip paṇṭu tōṉṟiya moḻikaṭku atikārikaḷākār eṉpatait teṟṟeṉa vuṇarka.
par.te.po.mī.yiṉ tamiḻilakkaṇattil ayaṉmoḻi yamaippukaḷ (Foreign Models in Tamil Grammar) eṉṉum tamiḻilakkaṇa ārāycci nūl, 9 atikāraṅkaḷum 4 piṉṉuraikaḷumāka 13 kaṭṭuraikaḷaik   koṇṭatu.  avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu, tamiḻilakkaṇa varalāṟu' (A History of Tamil Grammar) eṉṉun talaippaiyuṭaiyatu.
tamiḻilakkaṇa varalāṟu
itil, āciriyar tam ārāycci nūl muḻumaikkum muṉṉuraiyākat tolkāppiyam mutal iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil māṇavarkku eḻutappaṭṭa urainaṭai yilakkaṇac cuvaṭikaḷ varai, iṟṟait  tami ḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷaiyellām perumpāluṅ kālamuṟaippaṭi eṭuttuk kūṟi, avaṟṟiṉ āciriyar, uraiyāciriyar, puravalar, marapuccārpu, ilakkaṇap pirivu.  moḻinaṭai mutaliya kuṟippukaḷaiyum curukkamākak kuṟittiruk kiṉṟār.
tamiḻilakkaṇattai āriyac cārpiṉatākak kāṭṭuvatē par. te.po.mī.yiṉ aṭippaṭaik kuṟikkōḷātalāl, tolkāppiya marapu, naṉṉūl marapu, camaṟkiruta marapu eṉat tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷai mūvēṟu marapiṉavāka vakuttuk kūṟuvatai mutaṉmaiyākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟār.  iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭa vakuttiruppiṉum, mutaliraṇṭum aṭippaṭaiyil allatu orumaruṅku  camaṟkirutac cārpeṉṟum, iṟutiyatu muṟṟum camaṟ kirutac  cārpeṉṟum, koḷvatē avar karuttu. itu tolkāppiyam pāṇiṉīya vaḻiyateṉṟu piṉṉark kūṟuvatāl teriyavarum.
tamiḻilakkaṇam taṉippaṭṭateṉṟum iṉṟum oppuyarvaṟṟa teṉṟum aṟitaṟku, ataṉ poruḷatikāramē pōtiya cāṉṟām. camaṟkirutattiṟ poruḷilakkaṇa miṉmaiyāl, collatikāramē ataṟkuc ciṟantatākak koḷḷappaṭum. tamiḻilō, poruḷilakkaṇamē ilakkaṇa meṉṟum, atai yaṟitaṟkuk karuviyē eḻuttuñ  collumeṉṟum koḷḷappaṭum.
aracaṉ, iṉi nāṭu nāṭāyiṟṟākaliṉ nūl vallāraik koṇarka veṉṟu ellāp pakkamum āṭpōkka, eḻuttatikāramuñ collatikāra mum vallārait talaippaṭṭuk koṇarntu, poruḷatikāram vallārai eṅkun talaippaṭṭilē meṉṟu vantār. vara, aracaṉum puṭai paṭak kavaṉṟu, eṉṉai? eḻuttuñ collum ārāyvatu poruḷati kārattiṉ poruṭṭaṉṟē! poruḷatikāram peṟēmeṉiṉ, ivai peṟṟum peṟṟilēm eṉac collā niṟpaeṉṟu iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷurai uraittal kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum, iṟṟai akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ ilakkaṇa vilakkiyamellāṅ kaṟṟum, tamiḻiṉ perumaiyaiyum ataṉ ilakkaṇa vuyarvaiyum iṟṟait tamiḻp pērāciriyar palarum uṇarātiruppataṟku, avar muṉṉōr mūvāyira vāṭṭai yāriya vaṭimaittaṉattil uḻaṉṟa taṉāṟ pakuttaṟi viḻantamaiyē kāraṇamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, oru taṉippaṭṭavar allatu cārār uyarnilai yaṭain tiruppiṉ, an nilai toṉṟutoṭṭateṉṟē tuṇiya   viyalātu. kaḷavu ceytum piṟarporuḷ paṟittum avar an nilai yaṭaintirukkalām.  atai varalāṟu koṇṭu aṟital vēṇṭum.
āṅkilar 1765-l nīrāvip poṟi puṉaiyumuṉ ilakkiyattil  tamiḻariṉum tāḻntaṉṟi uyarntiruntatillai. aṅṅaṉamē, intiya āriyarum tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoḷḷu muṉ, ciṟuteyva  vaḻuttut tiraṭṭākiya vētantavirat tamiḻarpōl ilakkiya vilakkaṇam uṭaiyarā yiruntatillai. iṉṟō, ``kuppai yuyarntatu,  kōpuran tāḻntatu'' eṉṉum nilaimai ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu.
mēlum, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr moḻiyum oruvakait tamiḻt tiripē eṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
par. te.po.mī. tamiḻāriya moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟattaiyum neṭ ṭiṭaippaṭṭa muṉmai piṉmaiyaiyum teṉmai vaṭamaiyaiyum ārāyntaṟiyāmaiyāl, av virumoḻikaḷum iṟṟai nilaiyilēyē toṉṟum iruntatākak koṇṭu, tolkāppiyamē tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūleṉṟum, atu pāṇiṉīyattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭateṉṟum kiṟittava vūḻit toṭakkak kālattiṉa teṉṟum, atiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa āṟṟuppaṭai yilakkaṇam pattuppāṭṭu  āṟṟuppaṭaikaḷaiyē mūla ilakkiyamākak koṇṭataṉāl ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya piṟkālat tol kāppiyattaic cērntateṉṟum, paṉṉirupaṭala āciriyar paṉṉiru variṉ āciriyar oruvarē akattiyareṉṟum, avar uṭampaṭumeypaṟṟi muṟkālat tolkāppiyaroṭu karuttu vēṟupaṭukiṟāreṉṟum, tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellām palapaṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻ ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyātalāl, ataṉ mutaṟkāla ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmutu paḻaimaiyaṉa; eṇṇiṟantaṉa; peyarun teriyāvāṟu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyira āciriyarāṉa paṉampāraṉār,
centamiḻ vaḻakkoṭu civaṇiya nilattu
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval
eṉṟu, tolkāppiya mutaṉūlkaḷ palaveṉap potuppaṭak kuṟittār.
tolkāppiya mutal nūṟpāvē, eḻutteṉap paṭupa, muppaḵ teṉpa eṉṟu muṉṉūlaiyum muṉṉūlāciriyariṉ paṉmaiyaiyum kuṟik kiṉṟatu.  ātalāl, tolkāppiyam cārpiṟ cārpāṉa paṇṭaip paṉṉūl tokuppēyaṉṟip putuppaṭat tōṉṟiya taṉinūlaṉṟu.
pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varaippiṉ
nāṟpē rellai yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar. 	(tol.cey.78)
āciriyam vañci veṇpāk kaliyeṉa
nāliyaṟ ṟeṉpa pāvakai viriyē 	(cey.104)
maruṭpā paripāṭ ṭeṉaviru vakaiyum
maruviya kalavaip pāveṉa maruvum
vaṇṇan tāṉē nālain teṉpa.  	(cey.204)
vaṉappiyal tāṉē vakukkuṅ kālai
ammai aḻaku toṉmai tōlē
viruntē iyaipu pulaṉē iḻaipeṉa
eṇvakai yāmeṉ ṟiyampiṉar pulavar.
pāṭṭiṭaik kalanta poruḷa vākip
pāṭṭi ṉiyala paṇṇatti yiyalpē.	(cey.178)
iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa yāppiyaṟ kūṟukaṭkum eṭuttuk kāṭṭilakkiyam, tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatē yaṉṟip  pintiyataṉ ṟeṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
iyaipu eṉṉum vaṉappiṟkuc cilappatikāramum maṇimēkalaiyumē mūla ilakkiyam eṉpataṟkum iṭamillai. av viru pāviyaṅkaḷum ṉakara mey yoṉṟaiyē pāṭṭiṟutiyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra vīṟṟup
puḷḷi yiṟuti iyaipeṉap paṭumē	(tol.cey.238)
eṉpatu iyaipilakkaṇa mātalāl, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ eṉṉum aimmelliṉa vīṟṟilum iṟṟa pāṭṭukaḷaik koṇṭa aivakai iyaipu   vaṉappiṟkum mūlavilakkiyam, tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatākavē iruttal vēṇṭum. atupōṉṟē,
kūttarum pāṇarum porunarum viṟaliyum
āṟṟiṭaik kāṭci yuṟaḻat tōṉṟip
peṟṟa peruvaḷam peṟāark kaṟivuṟīic
ceṉṟupayaṉ etirac coṉṉa pakkamum	(tol. puṟat. 36)
eṉṉum āṟṟuppaṭai yilakkaṇattiṟkum mūla ilakkiyam, tol kāppiyattiṟku muntiyatē yeṉpatu tāṉē peṟappaṭum. 
pāṇiṉikku muntiya ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭut tolkāppiyarum, ki.pi.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭupō liyaṟṟappaṭṭa paṉṉirupaṭala āciriyaruḷ oruvarāṉa tolkāppiyarum, āka iruvar tolkāppiyar eṉṟu koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.  āyiṉ, iru tolkāppiyam iruntatillai. muṟkālat tolkāppiyar eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟum paṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇam  muḻumaiyum tokuttavar. an nūlē tol kāppiyam eṉa aṉṟirun tiṉṟuvarai vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. piṟkālat tolkāppiyar, par. te.po.mī. kūṟukiṉṟavāṟē, paṉṉirupaṭalattuḷ mutalatākiya veṭcip paṭalattai iyaṟṟiyavar. āṟṟuppaṭaiyilakkaṇam poruḷatikārattil pāṭāṇtiṇaik kuriyatēyaṉṟi veṭcit tiṇaik kuriyataṉṟu. iṉi, veṭcippaṭalam iyaṟṟiyavarē pāṭāṇ paṭalamum iyaṟṟi yirukkavum muṭiyātu; orukāl iyaṟṟiyiruppiṉum ataip pulavar tolkāppiyattōṭu mayakkiyirukkavum muṭiyātu. iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷoṭu nampiyakapporuḷai mayakkuvārillai. atupōṉṟē ituvum.
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇam iyampaliṉ	(naṉ. 141)
ilakkiya miṉṟi yilakkaṇa miṉṟē	(pērakat.)
eṉpataṉāl, ilakkaṇa nūlākiya tolkāppiyattiṟku muṉ ettuṇaiyō ilakkiya vaṉappukaḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṉit tolkāppiyattil cila āriyak karuttukaḷum kuṟippukaḷu miruppatāl, taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām ataṟku muṟpaṭṭē yiruntu iyaṟkaiyālum ceyaṟkaiyālum aḻiyuṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
āyiṉ par. te.po.mī. tolkāppiyattaiyē tamiḻ mutaṉūlāka vum, āṟṟuppaṭai yilakkaṇattaip pattuppāṭṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatākavum, tolkāppiyarai akattiyarukku muntiyavarākavum, kāṭṭiyiruppatāl, avar kālattiṟkumuṉ mūvēntarāṭciyum pulavar pāṇar kūttar porunar viṟaliyar mutaliyōr vāḻkkaiyum, tamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇamum tamiḻa nākarikamum iruntatillaiyeṉṟē, ārāycciyillār karuta vaittuḷ ḷār. im mayakkai aṟavē maṟuttaṟkup pāṭṭurai nūlē eṉṟa tol kāppiya nūṟpā  oṉṟē pōtiyatākum.
tolkāppiyar pōṉṟē akattiyarum iruvarāvar. mutalakattiyar vāṉmīkiyār kālattavar. avarē mutaṉmutalākat teṉṉāṭu  vanta āriyar. ataṉāl, paṉimalai yeḻucciyum kumarimalai muḻukkum  avar varavoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttik katai kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. avar muttamiḻuṅ kaṟṟut tam peyaril oru muttamiḻilakkaṇa nūliyaṟṟiṉā reṉpatu, vīramā muṉivar ceytiyai nōkkiṉ aiyuṟaviṟ kiṭantaruvataṉṟu. am muttamiḻakattiyam iṟantupaṭṭatu.
eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyicai koṇṭāṉ	(āraṇiya. akat. 47)
eṉṟu kamparum pāṭutal kāṇka.
iraṇṭāvatu akattiyar, ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭup paṉṉirupaṭala āciriyaṉmāriṉ āciriyarā yiruntirukkalām. avariyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟami ḻilakkaṇa nūlākiya akattiyattiṉiṉṟē, uraiyāciriyaṉmār cila nūṟpākkaḷai eṭuttāṇṭuḷḷaṉar pōlum!
piṟkālap pērāciriyat tiraṭṭu eṉpatu ārāyccikkuriya taṉṟu.
īruyirolikaḷ iṭaiyīṭiṉṟi yolikkumpōtu, iṭaiyil yakara mey allatu vakaramey tōṉṟuvatu ellā moḻikaṭkum potu viyalpām. aṅṅaṉam tōṉṟum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉappaṭum. atai ellā moḻikaḷum eḻuttiṟ kuṟippatillai.
e-kā: 	maṇi + ōcai = maṇiyōcai		uṭampaṭumey kuṟikkappaṭum 
	pala + āṟu = palavāṟu	}	tamiḻ	     	
	my + object = my object, 		uṭampaṭumey kuṟikkappaṭā
	you + are = you are	}	āṅkilam
tamiḻilum uṭampaṭumey mutaṟkāla eḻuttiṟ kuṟikkappaṭātirun tirukkalām. oliyaṟivum meypporuḷaṟivum mikka piṟkālattil, atu kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ urupukoḷal varaiyār	(puṇariyal 38)
eṉpatu, uṭampaṭumeypaṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpā.
itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urai varumāṟu:
nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāyp puṇarum evvakai moḻik kum, uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭumeyyiṉatu vaṭivai  uyirīṟu kōṭalai nīkkār koḷvār āciriyar.
avai yakaramum vakaramum eṉpatu mutaṉūlpaṟṟik kōṭum;
uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram
uyirmutaṉ moḻivarūuṅ kālai yāṉa
eṉavum,
iṟutiyu mutalum uyirnilai variṉē
uṟumeṉa moḻipa uṭampaṭu meyyē
eṉavum kūṟiṉārākaliṉ.
mutalakattiyar pāratak kālattiṟku muntiya irāmāyaṇa kālattava rātalāl, ki.mu. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yiruntirukkalām; iraṇṭām akattiyar ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yiruntirukkalām; eṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. ākavē, ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa mutal tolkāppiyar av viruvarkkum iṭaippaṭṭavarāvār.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai teṉmai muṉmai mutaliya taṉmaikaḷ ataṉ tāymaiyaiyum talaimaiyaiyum kāṭṭuvatāl avaṟṟai maṟaittaṟ poruṭṭu, par. te.po.mī. mutalakattiyarait tamiḻoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttātu viṭṭuviṭṭu, tolkāppiya uṭampaṭumeyn nūṟpāvuraiyil nacciṉārk kiṉiyar mutaṉūl nūṟpākkaḷāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷ iraṇṭai yum, ōr akattiyaruṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu cāṉṟillātu koṇṭu, tol kāppiyar kālattil vaḻakkūṉṟāta uṭampaṭumeyyait tamkālattil vaḻakkūṉṟiyatākak kūṟum akattiyar tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavar eṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kumari nāṭṭu mutaṟkālat tamiḻil uṭampaṭumey eḻuttu moḻiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭātiruntirukkalāmēṉum, talaikkaḻakamum iṭaikkaḻakamun tāṇṭivanta tolkāppiyar kālattil atu vaḻakkūṉṟātirunta teṉpatu poruntātu.
mēlum, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal varaiyār  eṉṉum tolkāppiyar kūṟṟu, akkālattiṟ putitākat tamiḻ kaṟṟa āriyap pulavar cilariṉ tavaṟṟaik kaṇṭittatākavum irukkalām.
uṭampaṭumey koḷalvaraiyār eṉṟu kūṟātu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal varaiyār eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, uṭampaṭumey eḻuttu moḻiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭāviṭiṉum, olippumoḻiyil iyalpāka irukkavēyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṉum uṇmaiyai nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarttutal kāṇka.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, uṭampaṭumey nūṟpāvuraiyil tām eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya mēṟkōṭku āciriyar peyar allatu nūṟpeyar kuṟiyāmai maṭṭumaṉṟi, iruvēṟu mutaṉūliṉiṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭi yiruppatu par. te. po. mī. kūṟṟaip poruḷila tākkum.
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇip pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval eṉṉum ciṟappup pāyiramum, eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē, eṉamoḻipa, uṇaru mōrē varaiyār, eṉṟici ṉōrē eṉṟellām āciriyar neṭukaluṅ kūṟic cellum peruvaḻakkum, tolkāppiya mutaṉūlkaḷiṉ paṉmaiyai maṭṭumaṉṟi, avaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟiṉ peyar aṟiyappaṭātu pōṉamaiyaiyum kuṟippāyt terivikkum.
iṉi,
viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉilai moḻikkum
niṉaiyuṅ kālai avvakai varaiyār	(tol. uyir. 62)
koṭimuṉ variṉē aiavaṇ niṟpak
kaṭinilai yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti	(tol.uyir. 83)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷai nōkkiṉ, varaiyār eṉpatu muṟkāla nilaimai yuṇarttum oru ciṟappuk kuṟippuc collaṉṟeṉpatum tōṉṟum. ataṉāl, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, nīkkār koḷvar eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟi yamaintār.
tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiya mūla nūlkaḷellām uṭampaṭu meyyait tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟiyiruppatāl, tolkāppiyamum attakaiyatēyeṉak koḷvatē takkatām. kāl māttirai yolikkum kakarak kuṟukkattaiyum makarak kuṟukkattaiyum nuṇṇitā yuṇarnta muṉṉait tamiḻaṟiñar, uṭampaṭumey kaṇṭaṟintāreṉpatu immiyum viyattaṟkuriyataṉṟu.
cēṉāvaraiyar, peruntēvaṉār mutaliya uraiyāciriyarum, puttamittiraṉ, cuppiramaṇiya tīṭcitar, cuvāmināta tēcikar, vaiyāpurip piḷḷai, par. te. po. mī. mutaliya nūlāciriyarum, koṇṭa camaṟkiruta mayakkamum tamiḻikaḻvum aṭutta kaṭṭurai maṟuppukaḷil teḷivāka viḷakkappeṟum
2ām atikāram
kūṟṟu: tolkāppiyak kālam (Age of Tolkappiam)
tolkāppiyamum camaṟkirutamum
tolkāppiyam, camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ tirikkappaṭṭut tamiḻilakki yattil vaḻaṅki vantatai nōkkukiṉṟatu. (tol. 880, 884, 885. kaḻakap patippu, 1954). tolkāppiyarē camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷai āṇṭiruppataip pirayōka vivēkam kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. ēṟkeṉavē vēṟōriṭattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷavāṟu karuttuvēṟupāṭuka ḷiruntaṉa vēṉum, oliyiyalum ilakkaṇamumpaṟṟi ōr aṉaittintiyak koḷkaiyiruntatu. tol kāppiyar mēlaiyar koḷkaiyaiyum cīṉar koḷkaiyaiyum piṉpaṟṟātu av vaṉaittintiyak koḷkaiyaiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉār eṉpatupaṟṟik karuttuvēṟupā ṭirukkamuṭiyātu. camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ  amaippu muṟai ēṟkeṉavē vēṟoru kaṭṭuraiyil āyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. av veḻuttukaḷiṉ teḷḷiya olippumuṟai ikkāla olinūlai niṉaivuṟutti ṉum, intiyāviṟkē ciṟappāka vuriyatākum. avai yāvum moḻikaḷiṉ ulakap potuviyalpukaḷ tolkāppiyar avaṟṟai uṇarntukoṇṭār. ataṉāl, avar aṉaittintiyak koḷkaiyaip piṉpaṟṟuvatil tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpaik keṭukkavillai.
vaṭakkoṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ
ivai yāyum, camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇa muṟaimaiyoṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpukoṇṭa piṉṉarē tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatai meyppikkuñ cāṉṟukaḷākiṉṟaṉa. mēlaiyar 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ camaṟ kiruta ilakkaṇattaik kaṇṭupiṭittatē mēṟkil moḻinūl vaḷarccikku vaḻikāṭṭiyatu pōṉṟu, kiḻakkil camaṟkirutattōṭum tiraviṭamallāta moḻikaḷōṭum toṭarpukoṇṭatē, tamiḻiṉ oliyiyalaiyum ilakkaṇa amaippaiyum vaṇṇikkum muyaṟciyai mēṟkoḷḷa nēraṭiyāṉa tūṇṭukōlā yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itu iyaṟkaitāṉē kaiyāḷum iṉakkalappup pōṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē ulakameṅkum nākarikaṅkaḷum kalaippaṇpāṭukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. im muyaṟciyum mutaliyaṉmai yuḷḷatē.
ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kāttiyāyaṉar
tiraviṭar irukku vētak kālattil āriyaroṭu toṭarpu koṇ ṭirukkalām. āṉāl, camaṟkirutattiṟkut tamiḻ nākarikak kalaip paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa toṉṟākaiyāl pintiyē nērntiruttal vēṇṭum.  caṭaṅkumuṟaimaikkum piṟa payaṉpāṭ ṭiṟkum vētaṅkaḷ tōrā. ki.mu. 1000 pōl tokukkappaṭṭatākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. vētamantiraṅkaḷiṉ oliyiyal ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷaip pōṟṟik kāttaṟkut tōtākap palvēṟu payiṟcikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ viḷaivāka, ilakkaṇamum oliyiyalum colliyalum coṟṟoṭariyalum vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇamuṟai niṟaivu peṟap pala talaimuṟaik  kālañ ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. pāṇiṉi         kālam ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu caṟṟu muṉpu eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. pāṇiṉikkut tamiḻaroṭu allatu avar kalaippaṇpāṭṭoṭu ētēṉum toṭarpiruntatākap pāṇiṉi moḻiyil yātoru kuṟippu millai. ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa vararuci eṉṉum kāttiyāyaṉar, pāṇiṉi nūṟpākkaḷiṉ kuṟai nirappalākiya vārttikaṅkaḷ eṉṉum tiruttavuraiyai iyaṟṟiṉār. avar tamiḻaraiyum avar kalaip paṇpāṭṭaiyum naṉṟāy aṟintiruntār. avar vārttikaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu cōḻa pāṇṭiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. cōḻapāṇṭiyam eṉpatu, kurupāñcālam eṉpatu pōṉṟē aracaraiyum avar nāṭṭaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. ātalāl, ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil allatu orukāl 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tolaiviluḷḷa vaṭavarkkum teṉṟicait tamiḻarkkum iṭaiyē kalaip paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpukaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭain tirukkalām. āṉāl, tolkāppiyam neruṅkiya toṭarpai atiṉum pēraḷaviṟ kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, oruvar tolkāppiyattaik ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muntiyatākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. (pak. 17-18).
maṟuppu
iyaṟcol tiricol ticaiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉaittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē	(tol. ec. 1)
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē 	(tol.ec. 5)
citaintaṉa variṉum iyaintaṉa varaiyār	(tol. ec. 6)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ, pāṇṭiyariṉ iraṇṭām talainakar muḻuki iraṇṭām tamiḻk kaḻakamum kulaintu, tūya tamiḻk kāvalariṉṟit tamiḻ āriyar vayappaṭṭuppōṉa kālattil, āriyam vērūṉṟiya cēra nāṭṭil vāḻnta tolkāppiyar, centamiḻ marapaiyum col vaḷattaiyum oruṅkē citaittu nāḷaṭaivil tamiḻai āriya vaṇṇa mākkum poruṭṭu, āriyat tamiḻp pakaivar tamiḻil vēṇṭātu pukuttiya orucila toṭakka vaṭacoṟkaḷai ayaṟcoṟkaḷeṉṟa vakaiyil kuṟittataik kāṭṭumēyaṉṟi, ac coṟkaḷ tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa veṉṟum, avaṟṟāl tamiḻ vaḷam peṟṟateṉṟum, uṇarttā.
pirāmaṇiyam tamiḻariṉ tāymoḻip paṟṟaiyum uṇarcciyaiyum perumpāluṅ koṉṟuviṭṭataṉāl, ayaṉmoḻiyāriṉ toṭarpu koḷḷumpōtellām orucila ayaṟcoṟkaḷaiyum vaḻakkiṟ pukuttik koṇṭaṉar. caṉṉal (jaṉṉal), cāvi eṉpaṉa pōrttukkīciyac coṟkaḷ. āyiṉ, avai tamiḻccoṟkaḷai vaḻakku vīḻttit tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ pōṉṟē vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu palakaṇi,  cāḷaram, kālatar eṉṉum mūṉṟu aḻakiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷaiyum, piṉṉatu tiṟavukōl, tuṟappukkuccu, tāḻakkōl eṉṉum mūṉṟu teḷiporuṭ coṟkaḷaiyum, ilakkiyac collum aruñcollum ākkiyuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, pōrttukkīciyac coṟkaḷ tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa veṉṟum, pōrttukkīciyamē tamiḻukku mūlameṉṟum, oruvarum collat tuṇiyār. iṅṅaṉamē vaṭacol nōkkiyum uṇarka.
pōrttukkīciyac colliṟkum vaṭacolliṟkum vēṟupā ṭeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, muṉṉatu taṉṉavarē pukuttikkoṇṭateṉṟum, piṉṉatu vēṟṟārum māṟṟārumāṉa āriyar pukuttiyateṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
tolkāppiyar tāmē āṇṭaṉavākap pirayōka vivēkam kuṟikkum vaṭacoṟkaḷ aṭutta kaṭṭuraiyil viḷakkappaṭum.
tamiḻ vērccollārāycciyum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum āriyaṉ  tōṉṟiyakamum paṟṟiya ārāycciyum tamakkiṉmaiyāl, par.te.po.mī. tamiḻarum āriyarum toṭarpaṟṟa vevvēṟiṉattā reṉṟum, orē kālattil tōṉṟiyavareṉṟum, iṉṟu pōṉṟē  muṉpum iruntaṉa reṉṟum, āriyarālēyē tamiḻar nākarikappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa reṉṟum, eṇṇikkoṇṭiruppatākat terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻar eṉṟatu tiraviṭaraiyum taḻuvum.
avar maturaip palkalaikkaḻakat tuṇaivēntarāyirunta kālattil, kumarimuṉaikkut teṟkē orukālattil nilamaṭṭum iruntateṉṟum, atil makkaḷ kuṭiyirukkavillaiyeṉṟum, oru muṟai coṉṉatākap par.me. cuntaraṉār coṉṉār. tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr kumarināṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatu, tamiḻ allatu tamiḻar varalāṟṟiṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa uyirnāṭic ceytiyākum. itai yuṇarātār ettuṇai kaṟpiṉum tamiḻaṟintavarākār.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟicai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi	(cilap. 11 : 19-22)
eṉṉum pakutiyum ataṟku aṭiyārkkunallār varainta uraiyumē, tamiḻaṉ kumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattiṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. iḵtallātu irupatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ēṉaic cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻarē, tamiḻiṉ palvēṟu nilaiyil palvēṟu ticai ceṉṟu ulaka muḻutum paraviṉar. (V.R.) irāmaccantira tīṭcitariṉ tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum (Origin and Spread of the Tamils) eṉṉum āṅkila nūlaip pārkka, vaṭapāṟ ceṉṟa tamiḻar tiraviṭarāyiṉar. vaṭa tiraviṭar pirākirutarāyiṉar. pirākirutar airōppāvum mēlāciyāvum ceṉṟu āriyarāyiṉar.
“First as to the Aryan family, called also Indo-European, which takes in the languages of part of South and West Asia, and almost the whole of Europe. The original tongue whence these are all descended may be called the Primitive Aryan. What the roots of this ancient         language were like, and how they were put together into words, the student may gain an idea from Greek and Latin, but a still better from Sanskrit, where both roots and inflexions have been kept up in a more perfect and regular state......
“.....The early home of the Aryans is supposed to have been in Inner Asia, perhaps in the present Turkestan, in the region of the Oxus and Yaxartes, for here the practicable way of migration for nomads with flocks and herds lies open down into Persia on the one side, and India on the other. As India and Persia have preserved in their sacred languages the Aryan tongue less changed than else where, it may be judged that the land whence the invading Aryans, came was not far off.”(Anthropology by Edward. B. Tylor, pp.156-7)
‘The Cradle of the Aryas’
“The fact....is in perfect harmony with the theory that the original home of the Aryas was on the slopes of the mountains which form  the junction between the Hindukush and the Karakorum chains, what may be called the Northern Kohistan.
“........the region drained by the feeders of the Indus, the Oxus, and Yaxtartes.” (Three Lectures on the Science of Language by F. Max Muller, pp. 64-5).
in naṭumēlai āciyāviṉiṉṟē vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr, kirēkkattiṟku neruṅkiya oru moḻiyaip pēcikkoṇṭum, āṭu māṭu mēyttukkoṇṭum, māṭṭiṟaicci yuṇṭukoṇṭum, iyaṟkaiyaiyum aimpūtaṅkaḷaiyum iru cuṭaraiyum vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭum, eḻuttum ilakkiyamu miṉṟi mullai nākarika nilaiyiṟ ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭamākak ki.mu. 2000-1500 pōl intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar.
avar moḻi kirēkkattiṟku neruṅkiya teṉpatu, kīḻvarum tā eṉṉum viṉaimuṟṟiṉ nikaḻkāla vaṭivoppumaiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.

tā eṉṉum ōr aṭippaṭait tamiḻ viṉaiccol, ilattīṉilum kirēkkattilum vētamoḻiyilum palvēṟu vaṭivil tirintu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉ mutaṉmaiyaiyum mūlat taṉmaiyaiyum kāṭṭa iḵtoṉṟē pōtum. ataṉāl, itai maṟukkumāṟu, īrāṇṭiṟku muṉpē par.te. po. mī. avarkaṭku ōr aṟaikūval viṭuttēṉ. ataṟku ituvarai maṟumoḻi yillai.
kīḻaiyāriyar kālnaṭai mēyttalaic ciṟapput toḻilākak koṇ ṭiruntaṉareṉpatu, kōttiram eṉṉum kuṭumpap peyarālum tuhitru eṉṉum makaṭpeyarālum, uḻavuttoḻilai mēṟkoḷḷavillai yeṉpatu, avar nāṭōṭit taṉmaiyālum,  mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷi luḷḷa ēr eṉṉun teṉcol avar moḻiyi liṉmaiyālum, uḻavut toḻilaik kolaittoḻileṉṟu avar taruma cāttiram paḻippatālum; aṟiyappaṭum. (kō = āṉ (pacu), tuh = kaṟa) avar moḻikku mēṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya peyar maṟaintoḻintatu. āriyam, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum avar intiyāviṟku vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē tōṉṟiyavai. āriyam eṉṉum col intiyāviṉiṉṟē mēlai nāṭukaṭkup paraviṟṟu.
āriyam eṉpatu tamiḻc collē. aru -āri = arumai, aḻaku, mēṉmai. āri yākavañ cāntan taṉittapiṉ (cīvaka. 129) āri - āriyaṉ = mēlōṉ. itu āriyarē tamiḻarai  ēmāṟṟit tamakku iṭṭukkoṇṭatu.  ‘aristos’ (best) eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṟkum aru eṉpatē mūlamāyiruttal vēṇṭum. ēr-Ar-Arya eṉpatu tavaṟu.
kum - cum - cam(sam). kummutal = kuvital, kūṭutal, kalattal, karumai = mikuti, perumai. kaḷḷutal = kalattal, kūṭutal. kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭṭam, avai. kaḷ - kar - karu. karuttal = mikutal, mikuttal, ceytal. karu - karumam. karu - karuvi. karu - karaṇam. karu(va.) kru - kruta. samkruta - samskruta = pirākirutamum tamiḻum, allatu vētamoḻiyum tamiḻum kalantu ceyyappaṭṭa moḻi. prākruta camaṟkirutattiṟku muntu ceyyappaṭṭa moḻi. putitākac ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷāl ap poru ḷiṉam  mikutal kāṇka. iṉi, karuttal = kai karuttuk kāḻppēṟu maḷavu uḻaittuc ceytal eṉṟumām. karuṅkaik kollar (cilap. 5:29) eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. karuttal eṉṉum viṉai tamiḻil muṉpē vaḻakkaṟṟatu. pirā = muṉ. kruta = ceyyappaṭṭatu
tamiḻar ki.mu. 100,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarināṭṭil  tōṉṟiya mūla makkaḷiṉattāriṉ vaḻivantavar. iṟṟai ilakkiyattiṉum virivāṉa, eḻunilac ceyyuḷāliyaṉṟa paltuṟai muḻut tūya ilakkiyattaiyum muttamiḻilakkaṇattaiyum koṇṭiruntavar.

mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya piṇṭat tāṉumeṉ
ṟāṅkaṉai marapi ṉiyalu meṉpa.	(cey. 166)
nēriṉa maṇiyai niralpaṭa vaittāṅ
kōriṉap poruḷai yoruvaḻi vaippa
tōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar. 	(cey. 168)
oruneṟi yiṉṟi viraviya poruḷāṟ
potumoḻi toṭariṉatu paṭala mākum. 		(cey. 169)
mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya taṉmait tāyiṟ
ṟōṉṟumoḻip pulavar piṇṭa meṉpa 	(cey. 170)
eṉa viḷakki, īruṟuppaṭakkiya nūl eṉṟum, mūvuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭam eṉṟum, nūlai iruvakaiyāka vakuttuk kūṟiṟṟu.
mēṟkūṟiya piṇṭa nūṟpāviṟku.
am mūṉṟu uṟuppiṉaiyum aṭakki varuvatu piṇṭam eṉṟavāṟu.
 am mūṉṟaṉaiyum uṟuppeṉavē, piṇṭameṉpaṉatām uṟuppiṉaveṉpatu peṟṟām. tolkāppiyam eṉpatu piṇṭam;  ataṉuḷ eḻuttatikāram, collatikāram, poruḷatikāra meṉpaṉa paṭalameṉap paṭum; avaṟṟuḷ ōttuñ cūttiramum oḻinta iru kūṟumeṉappaṭum.
tōṉṟumoḻip pulavar piṇṭa meṉpa eṉṟataṉāl, piṇṭat tiṉaiyum aṭakkiniṟpatu vēṟu piṇṭamuḷateṉpatu. atu mutaṉūlākiya akattiyamē pōlum; eṉṉai? aḵtu iyaṟṟamiḻ, icaittamiḻ, nāṭakat tamiḻeṉṉum mūṉṟu piṇṭattiṉaiyum aṭakki niṟṟaliṉ eṉṟu uraittār pērāciriyar.
ottu eṉiṉum iyal eṉiṉum okkum. atikāram eṉiṉum paṭalam eṉiṉum pāl eṉiṉum okkum.
pāṇiṉīyam cūttiram, pātam, attiyāyam eṉṉum mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiyatēṉum ataip piṇṭameṉṉum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyilillai. mēlum, atu eḻuttum collum ākiya iraṇṭē kūṟuvatāl, īruṟup paṭakkiyatākavē koḷḷappaṭum. ātalāl, atu nūlaṉṟip piṇṭamākātu.
tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa poruḷatikāram eṉṉum mūṉṟā muṟuppu, yāppu, aṇiyeṉṉum potuvakaik kūṟukaḷoṭu vēṟem moḻiyilu millāta poruḷilakkaṇa meṉṉum taṉicciṟappuk kūṟṟaik koṇ ṭiruttalāl, tolkāppiyattuk kiṇaiyāṉa ilakkaṇanūl iv vulakil oṉṟumē yillaiyeṉpatu teḷḷat teḷivām. poruḷilakkaṇattaip pāṭṭiyaleṉṟu kūṟi ataṉ perumaiyaik kuṟaikkavum maṟaikkavum muyalpavar tam aḻukkāṟṟiṉ aḷaviṉmaiyaik kāṭṭuvatōṭu, nēr niṉṟu karuṅkākkaiyai veṇpuṟā veṉṉum kaṭuntuṇiccaṟ poyyarē yāvar.
iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷurai kūṟum toṉṉūlkaḷai nampātum, tolkāppiyar āṅkāṅkuk kuṟippiṭum muṉṉūlkaḷaik karutātum, vaṭanūlkaḷilillāta tolkāppiyap poruḷilakkaṇattaik kaṇṭuṅ kāṇātatupōṟ kaṇmūṭiyum,  naṉṉūlum ciṉṉūlumpōl eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟum pāṇiṉīyattiṉiṉṟu mūṉṟuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭamāṉa tolkāppiyan tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, pūṉai vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu pulikkuṭṭi  vantateṉṟum, narimā arimāvai īṉṟateṉṟum kūṟuvatu pōṉṟatē.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakaiyeḻuttuk koṇṭa neṭuṅkaṇakku, intiyāvil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakilēyē mutaṉmutal tamiḻiltāṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataip piṉpaṟṟiyē pirāmiyum kirantamum tēvanākariyum tōṉṟiṉa. itu piṉṉoru kaṭṭuraiyil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
vētamantiraṅkaṭkuc ciṭcai eṉṉum oliyiyalum, pirāticākkiyam eṉṉum taṉimaṟai allatu mantirat tokutikkuriya ilakkaṇa muṟaiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇattaip piṉpaṟṟiyē eḻuntaṉa.
intiya āriyariṉ vaḻakkiṟanta tāymoḻiyum pirākirutamuṅ kalantatē vētamoḻi. vētamoḻiyum tamiḻum kalantatē camaṟkirutam eṉṉum palatuṟai ulakiyal ilakkiyamoḻi. vētamoḻiyaic camaṟ kirutam eṉpatu muṟkālappaṭuttam (Prochronism) eṉṉum vaḻuvākum.
camaṟkirutattiṟku eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamē viyākaraṇam. mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiya aintiram eṉṉum camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam, tamiḻakatti lēyē tōṉṟit tamiḻakattilēyē aḻikkappaṭṭatu. aintirattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatē aṣṭātyāyī eṉṉum pāṇiṉīyam. tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭavarātaliṉ aintiram maṭṭum kaṟṟiruntār. ataṉā lēyē, aintiram niṟainta tolkāppi yaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār pāyirattiṟ pāṭiṉār.
tolkāppiyar kālam ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; pāṇiṉi kālam ki.mu.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam tamiḻilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟēyaṉṟi, tamiḻilakkaṇam  vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟavillai.
eḻuttumuṟai, eḻuttuccāriyai, eḻuttuppiṟappiyal, uyirmeyt taṉivaṭivam, puṇariyalvirivu, collamaippu, colvakai, tokaitoṭar vakai, eṇvēṟṟumai, mutaliyavaṟṟil, vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇam tamiḻilak kaṇattait taḻuviyiruppatu, teṟṟeṉat tōṉṟum. tolkāppiyar mūvatikārattaiyum ovvoṉpatu iyalkaḷāka vakuttiruppatu pōṉṟu, pāṇiṉiyār eṇṇattiyāyattaiyum nannāṉku pātamāka amait tiruppatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu. pāṇiṉīyam paṭippaṭiyākat tiruntivanta pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaṭkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. ataṉ muṉṉūlkaḷ 60 eṉpar kā.cu. piḷḷaiyār.
patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil, cārls vilkiṉcu (Charles Wilkins), uvilliyam cōṉcu (William Jones) mutaliya  mēlaiyaṟiñarāṟ camaṟkirutam veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa piṉ, mēṉāṭṭār āriya oppiyaṉ moḻinūlai vaḷarttaṉar. āyiṉ, āriyattiṉ mūlamoḻiyai aṟiyā maiyāl, ammaṭṭil niṟuttikkoṇṭu ataṟku vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. āriyam tirimoḻiyāta lāl, ataṉ vāyilāka vērccoṟkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷaiyum mūlamoḻi tōṉṟiya vakaiyaiyum aṟiya muṭiyātu. avaṟṟai aṟivikkak kūṭiya iyaṉmoḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē. uṇmaiyāṉa moḻinūl, oppiyalum vaṇṇaṉaiyiyalum varalāṟṟiyalum oruṅkē koṇṭu koṭivaḻiyal (Geneological) muṟaippaṭṭatākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl eṉpatu ōr aṟiviya lākātu. itu piṉṉarum viḷakkappaṭum. 
camaṟkirutattaik kaṇṭupiṭittapiṉ mēṉāṭṭār oppiyaṉ moḻinūlai vaḷarttukkoṇṭatupōl, tamiḻarum pāṇiṉīyattaip piṉpaṟṟiyē tamiḻilakkaṇam amaittukkoṇṭaṉar eṉpatu, pēraṉē pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟatē. tolkāppiyar camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇamum ilakkiyamuṅ kaṟṟiruntār eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. āyiṉ. pāṇiṉīyattaip piṉpaṟṟit tolkāppiyattai iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu aṟiyāmai paṟṟiyatē. itai avar kūṟṟum poruḷatikāramum teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭuttum. ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ tōṉṟiya talaikkaḻakak kālattiliruntu eḻuttu col poruḷ eṉṉum mūvatikārap paṭṭu, yāppaiyum aṇiyaiyum poruḷuḷ aṭakki, oṇṭīntuṟai yeṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum viyakkumaḷavu nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakka akapporuḷ koṇṭu, marapāka vaḻaṅki vanta teyvat tamiḻilakkaṇattai, ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil tōṉṟi eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟum pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻiyatākak kūṟuvatu, tamiḻaik kāṭṭik koṭukkum ceyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭatēyaṉṟi vēṟākātu.
iṉi, vētak kālattil āriyar tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koḷḷa villaiyeṉṟum, kāttiyāyaṉar kālamākiya ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭi lēyē at toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟuvatum varalāṟṟoṭu muṟṟum muraṇpaṭṭatākum.
oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum tamiḻarē paraviyiruntaṉar. vaṭakkē cellac cella moḻiyum nāḷaṭaivil mella mellat tirintatu. ataṉāl, vaṭanāṭukaḷaip piṟkālattil moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar.
moḻipeyar paṉmalai yiṟappiṉum
oḻital cellā toṇṭoṭi kuṇaṉē. 	(aiṅ. 321)
moḻipeyar tēen tarumār maṉṉar
kaḻippiṇik kaṟaittōl niraikaṇ ṭaṉṉa. 	(akam. 67)
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum. 		(kuṟun. 11)
moḻimāṟu tēyam eṉṉātu moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟatai nōkkuka. peyartal = orē moḻi mella mellat tirital.
kumarimalaiyum paḵṟuḷiyāṟum mūḻkiyapiṉ, avaṟṟuk kīṭākap pāṇṭiyaṉ paṉimalaiyaiyum kaṅkaiyāṟṟaiyuṅ koṇṭāṉ. aṭiyārkkunallār ciṟappuk kuṟippaip pārkka (cilap. 11:17-22).
kumarināṭṭuc civaneṟiyār aṇintu vantatum nēpāḷa nāṭṭuc civamaṇiyē (uruttirākkamē).
vaṅka nāṭu ceṉṟu vāḻnta tamiḻa vaṇikarē, kāḷikōyil camaittuk kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉṟu nakarppeyariṭṭaṉar. aḵtu iṉṟu kalkattā eṉṟu āṅkilattil tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam (Tamluk) eṉṟoru nakarum an nāṭṭil tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilavāṇikamum nīr vāṇikamum vaṭanāṭukaḷoṭu toṭarntu nikaḻntuvantaṉa.
vaṭanāṭṭār kumariyāṭavum teṉṉāṭṭār kaṅkaiyāṭavum vantu celvatu, toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamā yiruntuvantatu. pāratak kālattil arccuṉaṉum kumariyāṭa vantu pāṇṭiyaṉ kumariyai maṇantāṉ.
vēta āriyaruḷ mutaṉ mutalāka vintiyamalai tāṇṭit teṉṉāṭu vanta āriya muṉivar akattiyar, ataṉāl vintamalaiyiṉ ceruk kaṭakkiyavar eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭār. avarukkup piṉ vantavar nāratar. piṉṉart takkaṇapatam eṉṉum oḻuṅkāṉa pātaiyai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar āriyar.
iyaṟkaiyaiyum aimpūtaṅkaḷaiyum irucuṭaraiyum āvikaḷaiyum cōmaccāṟāṉa kaḷḷaiyumē vaṇaṅkivanta vēta āriyar, tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭa piṉṉarē, kāḷi vaṇakkattaiyum kumara vaṇakkat         taiyum civatirumāl mataṅkaḷaiyum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ patiṉeṇ purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum upaniṭataṅkaḷum ākamaṅ  kaḷum kāviya nāṭakaṅkaḷum viyākaraṇaṅkaḷum nikaṇṭukaḷum cāttiraṅ kaḷum piṟavum eḻuntaṉa.
iṅṅaṉamirukkavum, tolkāppiyamē mutal tamiḻnūleṉṟum, atuvum pāṇiṉīyattait taḻuviyateṉṟum mutaṉmutalāka naḷiyiru munnīr nāvā yōṭṭic (puṟam. 66) cuṟṟuk kaṭalōṭiyāyt (circumna vigator) tikaḻntu vaṭavai (Aurora Borealis) kaṇṭa tamiḻaṉaik ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai vaṭaintiyāvum aṟiyātavaṉ eṉṟu, iṟappa iḻittuk kūṟuvatu iḷañciṟārum eḷḷi nakaiyāṭattakkatē.
kuṟippu: mūlat tamiḻ nāṭaka nūliṉiṉṟu moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa kācumīra  vaṭamoḻip parata cāttiram, maṟiniḻalīṭāka mīṇṭum mūla moḻi yāṉa tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkappaṭa virukkiṉṟatu. itaik kaṇṭu allatu kēṭṭu mūla moḻipaṟṟi mayaṅkaṟka.
nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiyam mutalākavuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷum iṟantaṉa eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār tam uraip pāyirattil, uraittiruppatāl, vaṭamoḻi moḻipeyarppiṉ mūlat tamiḻp paratam aḻikkappaṭṭateṉa uyttuṇarka. itu  eṉ muttamiḻ eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
kūṟṟu: acōkaṉum tamiḻnāṭum
cantirakuptaṉ kālattil, vitappāka acōkaṉ kālattē, (vaṭavar teṉṉavar) toṭarpu neruṅkiyiruntatu. acōkaṉ kaṭṭaḷai vācakaṅkaḷ piramakirivarai teṟkiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ viṭuceytikaḷ tamiḻaracar uṭpaṭa aṉaivarkkum viṭappaṭṭuḷa. avaroṭu toṭarpu neruṅkiyatāki yiruttal vēṇṭum. acōkaṉ cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, cattiyaputtirar allatu  atikamāṉar eṉṉum tamiḻnāṭṭaracar nālvaraip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. avaruḷ mutal mūvarē aracareṉṟum, atikamāṉar  cēraroṭu uṟavukoḷḷa muyaṉṟa ciṟṟaracarē yeṉṟum, tamiḻ marapuvaḻic ceyti kūṟukiṉṟatu. caṅkak kālattilum tolkāppiyattilum in nilai maiyē kāṇkiṉṟōm. atikamāṉar vallamaiyuḷḷavarāyiruntu ēṉai mūvaracaroṭum araciyal talaimaikkup pōṭṭiyiṭṭa kālam oṉṟu, ataṟku muṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. atu acōkaṉ kaṭṭaḷai vācakaṅkaḷ uruvāṉa kālamāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟai aṉaittintiya eḻuttumuṟaiyiṉ taḻuviya amaippē yeṉṟum, atait tolkāppiyar kuṟippiṭukiṟār eṉṟum, iṉṉoru kaṭṭuraiyiṟ collāṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. acōkaṉ viṭuceyti potumakaṉ uḷḷattaik kavarntataṉāl, avaṉukku eḻuttaṟiviṉmītu vēṭkai vaḷarntatu. atu aṉaittintiya veḻuttait taḻuvit tamiḻeḻuttai yamaittaṟku akat tūṇṭukōlāyiṟṟu allatu atai viraivupaṭuttiṟṟu. itu aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ, tolkāppiyam acōkaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉṉarē tōṉṟi yiruttal kūṭum.
puḷḷikkoḷkai
puḷḷikkoḷkai paṟṟiyum palaviṭattuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟkup piṉpumtāṉ puḷḷi tōṉṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟu  kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu. veṭṭeḻuttukaḷiṉ eḻuttu muṟaimai ilakkaṇak koḷkaiyaip piṉpaṟṟuvataṟku oru nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kālam ceṉṟirukku meṉṟu nām karutikkoḷḷalām. veṭṭeḻuttukaḷ potumakkaḷ ellārkkumeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭavai. potu makkaḷ vaḻakkaṅkaḷ eḷitil māṟuvatillai. ātalāl, tolkāppiyam kiṟittavavūḻit tiruppattil allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟu muntit tōṉṟiya tākak koḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, camaṟkirutattiṟ pōl eḻuttu muṟaimaikkut tēvaiyiṉṟiyē ilakkaṇak kūṟupaṭuppu vaḷarcci yaṭaintirukkak kūṭiyatē. iṉi,  eḻuttu muṟaiyaip paṟṟiya kuṟippuka ḷellām piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷ eṉpatum koḷḷakkūṭiyatē.
ilakkaṇam
āṉāl, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum atu kāttiyāyaṉār kālattukku muntiyatākātu. kāttiyāyaṉarē ilakkaṇam eṉṉum collāṭciyai, `lakṣya' allatu vaḻakku eṉpatai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa aṇaṅ kattaik (ilakkaṇattaik) kuṟittateṉṟu viḷakka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, tamiḻilakkaṇam tamiḻnāṭṭil ik kalviyiṉ neṭun toṉmai yaik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil vevvēṟu paṭaikaḷ
muṟkālattavar oruvarum piṟkālattavar oruvarumāka iruperu nūlāciriyar tolkāppiyar eṉṉum potuppeyar koṇṭiruntataṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa mayakkattaiyum kuḻappattaiyum yām ēṟkeṉavē viḷakki yirukkiṉṟēm. ik karuttuppaṭi mayakkam nērak kūṭiyatātal iṟṟait tolkāppiyattaik kavaṉamākak kūṟupaṭutti āyat tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. attakaik kūṟupāṭṭāyvutāṉ an nūliṉ palvēṟu paṭaikaḷai veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭavoṇṇum. ap paṭaikku vēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷ paṟṟik kālaṅ kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
acōkaṉ kālam ki.mu. 272-31. kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu mutaṟkāla makkaḷ vaṭakiḻakkum vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkumāka vaṭaāciyāviṟkum airōppā viṟkum ceṉṟapiṉ, avar vaḻiyiṉar intiyāvukkut tirumpi varu muṉṉum, vaṭaintiyāvarai tiraviṭar perumpāṉmaiyarākavum tamiḻar ciṟupāṉmaiyarākavum paraviyiruntaṉar. tamiḻarē tiraviṭarākavum tiraviṭarē pirākirutarākavum māṟiyataṉāl, āriyar vanta kālattil vaṭaintiyar perumpālum pirākirutarākavum, naṭuvintiyar perumpālum tiraviṭa rākavum iruntaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai, camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiṟ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum muttamiḻt teṉṉāṭṭaracukaḷē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa acōkaṉ kalveṭṭu kaḷil, pāṇṭiyam cōḻam kēraḷaputtiram (cēralam) eṉṉum muttamiḻ aracak kuṭikaḷuṭaṉ cattiyaputtirar eṉṉum vēṟōr araca marapiṉarum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ac cattiyaputtirarait takaṭūrait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭuvanta atikamāṉar eṉṉum ciṟṟaracak kuṭiyiṉarākap par. te. po. mī. kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
atikamāṉar oru paḻañcērar kiḷaiyaic cērnta ciṟṟaraca rātaliṉ, avarait taṉiyē pirittuk kūṟat tēvaiyillai. kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil mūvēntaruk kaṭuttapaṭiyāka vallamai yōṭiruntu, kōcar eṉṉum oru vakuppār tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭamēlaip pakutiyāṉa tuḷunāṭṭai āṇṭu vantatāka, eṭṭuttokaiyum pattuppāṭṭum cilappatikāramum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi (kuṟun. 15), oṉṟumoḻik kōcar (kuṟun. 73), meymmali perumpūṭ cemmaṟ kōcar (akam. 15), vaṭuvuṭai mukattar kaṭuṅkaṭ kōcar (akam. 90), kāppukkai niṟutta palvēṟ kōcar (akam. 113), oṉṟumoḻik kōcar (akam. 196), vāymoḻi nilaiiya cēṇviḷakku nallicai  vaḷaṅkeḻu kōcar (akam. 205), palliḷaṅ kōcar (akam. 216), velkoṭit tuṉaikā laṉṉa puṉaitērk kōcar (akam. 251), ūrmutu kōcar (akam. 262), veṉvēl iḷampal kōcar (puṟam. 169), valampuri kōcar (puṟam. 283), naṉaikkaḷḷiṉ maṉaik kōcar (puṟam. 369). avaiyakam viḷaṅka nāṉmoḻik kōcar (maturaik. 508-9), kaṭantaṭu vāyvā ḷiḷampal kōcar (maturaik. 773), atukēṭṭuk koṅkiḷaṅ kōcar taṅkaḷ nāṭṭakattu naṅkaikku viḻavoṭu cānti ceyya maḻaitoḻil eṉṟum māṟātāyiṟṟu. (cilap. uraipeṟu kaṭṭurai.)

eṉṉum tolkāppiya aṭiyāl viḷaṅkum.
acōkaṉ kālattil atikamāṉ kai tamiḻakattil ōṅkiyirunta teṉpataṟku yātoru cāṉṟumillai.
iṟṟait tamiḻp pulaviyattuḷ tolkāppiyamē mutaṟ pottakamā yiruttal paṟṟi, ārāycciyillārellārum atuvē mutal tamiḻnūl eṉa mayaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaiyāpuriyārō veṉiṉ, ataiyum pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻinūlākak kāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.
tolkāppiyam paṭukuḻikaḷum kavarneṟikaḷum niṟaintatu. ātalāl, māntaṉ varalāṟṟārāycciyum moḻivaralāṟṟārāycciyum tamiḻ vērccollārāycciyum illātavar, ki.mu.1200 pōl tōṉṟiya  ilakkiyat tirimoḻiyākiya camaṟkiruta aṟivaikkoṇṭu, ki.mu. nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya māntaṉ mutaṉbmāḻiyākiyatamiḻiyalpaiaṟiyappukuvatu,naḻikoṇṭuāḻiyaiaḷantaṟiyattuṇivatēbyākkum.acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceytikaḷāṟ kavarappaṭṭa tamiḻakap potu makkaḷ, avaṟṟait tāmē paṭittaṟiya vēṇṭumeṉṉum avāviṉāl untappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ pirāmiyeḻuttaip payiṉṟukoṇṭaṉa reṉṟum. atuvē tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṟṟameṉṟum, par. te.po.mī. tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattāṟ kaṇṭiruppatu, ākā! ettuṇai arumaiyāṉa uyttuṇarvu! itu viṇṇulakattum maṇṇulakattum vēṟevarkkum tōṉṟiyirukkak kūṭāta viḻuppula maṇikkaruttē! oru kallaṟait tōṭṭa vāyiliṉ mēl nāykaḷ pukaṟka! eṉṟu āṅkilattil eḻuti yirunta palakaiyaik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, oru nāy pukātu niṉṟuviṭṭa teṉṟu kūṟum pikkuvikkut tāḷkaḷai (Pickwick papers) viṭa itu nakaic cuvai viñciyatē! itaṉāl, tikkeṉcai (Dickens)  veṉṟuviṭṭār par. te. po. mī. yār eṉṟē collalām.
acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttait tamiḻp potumakkaḷ kaṟṟapiṉ, tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu, tamiḻai iḻittum paḻittum puṟakkaṇikka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttiruntālaṉṟi, tolkāppiyaṅ kaṟṟa evaruñ collat tuṇiyār.
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal	(eḻut. 15)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvukkup piṉ,
ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē	(eḻut 16)
eṉṟuḷḷa nūṟpā tolkāppiyap paṭukuḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. itu  piṉṉar vēṟoru kaṭṭuraiyil virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻutti ṉiṉṟu potumakkaḷ tōṟṟuvittateṉṉum muḻut tavaṟāṉa  karuttai aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittē, par. te.po.mī. mēṟkoṇṭu ārāyntu cel kiṉṟār. mutaṟ kōṇal muṟṟum kōṇal eṉṟavāṟu, avar ārāycci aṭippaṭait tavaṟṟiṉāl muṟṟun tavaṟāka muṭikiṉṟatu.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakai yeḻuttu vēṟupāṭum, avaṟṟukku varivaṭiva vēṟupāṭum uḷḷa neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟai ulakil mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu,  muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. meykkut taṉivaṭivillātu tōṉṟiya kukaik kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭataṉāl, tamiḻ meyyeḻuttiṟkum puḷḷikkuṟi ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, uṇmaikku muṟṟum  māṟākum.
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷai nalvaḻippaṭutta an nāṭṭu aracaṉukkē iyalumātalāl, acōkaṉ taṉ āṭciyellaikkuḷ poṟitta pāṟai, kukai, kampam ākiya mūviṭak kalveṭṭukaḷilum, taṉ  atikārattuk kuṭpaṭṭavarkkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyākavum uṭpaṭāta ellaippuṟa nāṭṭaracarkku naṭpu muṟaippaṭṭa nalluraiyākavum, tuṉpuṟuttāmaiyai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭa aṉpu neṟiyīṭukaḷaik kuṟittatu, tamiḻakattaip poṟuttavaraiyil aracarai nōkkiyatēyaṉṟip potumakkaḷai nōkkiyataṉṟu. ōr aracaṉ naṭpu  nilaiyil maṟṟōr aracaṉiṉ kuṭikaṭku nēraṭiyāka ētēṉum eḻutuvatu iyalpumaṉṟu; muṟaiyumaṉṟu.
kīḻvarum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceytiyai nōkkuka.
2ām pāṟaik kalveṭṭu
(uvilcaṉ (Wilson) moḻipeyarppu)
“In all the subjugated territories of the king Priyadasi, the beloved of the gods, and also in the hordering countires, as Choda, Palaya (or Paraya) Satyaputra, Keralaputra, Tambapani it is proclaimed, and Antiochus by name the Yona (or Yavana) raja and those princes who are nearer to or allied with that monarco, universally are apprised that two designs have been cherished by Priyadasi, one (design) regarding men, and one relating to animals; and whatever herbs are useful to men or useful to animals wherever there are none, such have been everywhere caused to be conveyed and planted and roots and fruits wherever there are none, such have been everywhere conveyed and planted; and on the roads wells have been caused to be dug, and trees have been planted for the respective enjoyment of animals and men.”
(Corpus Inscriptionum Indicarum, Vol I. pp. 117-8)
itil `Palaya' eṉṟatu pāṇṭiyaṉai; Tambapani (tāmpiraparaṇi) eṉṟatu ilaṅkaiyai. acōkaṉ kalveṭṭuc ceyti cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya cattiyaputtirarākiya aracaraiyē  nōkkiyateṉpatu. itaṉāl teḷḷat teḷivām. mēlum kalveṭṭup poṟikkappaṭṭatu vaṭavellaip puṟattilēyē yaṉṟit tamiḻakattuḷḷaṉṟu. ataṉāl tamiḻp potumakkaḷ ataṉai aṟintēyirār; kaṇṭirār eṉpataic collavē vēṇṭuva tiṉṟu.
oru moḻiyiṟ kuṟuṅkaṇakkākavēṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkākavēṉum vaṇṇamālai ēṟpaṭuvatu, aṟiñarālēyēyaṉṟik kallāp potu makkaḷālaṉṟu. ākavē, tamiḻap potumakkaḷ acōkaṉ kalveṭ ṭiṉiṉṟu tamiḻeḻuttaik kaṟṟukkoṇṭatu, muṭavaṉ muṇmaramēṟik komputtēṉaik koṇarnta ceytiyē yākum.
iṉi, eḻuttiṉṟiyē camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻilum tōṉṟiyirukkalā meṉṟum, tolkāppiyattiluḷḷa ilakkaṇak kuṟippukaḷellām, piṟkālattaṉaveṉṟum, par. te.po.mī. kūṟiyiruppatu, avarukkut tamiḻilakkaṇattaip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa karuttiṉmaiyait teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam eṉṉum col varalāṟu piṉṉart teri vikkappaṭum. 
kūṟṟu : tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram
I. nūṉmarapu
oliyaṉkaḷiṉ paṭṭi, avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu
tolkāppiyattiṉ mutaṟpākam eḻuttatikāram eṉappaṭum. ataṉ mutaṟpirivu nūṉmarapu, atāvatu tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiṉ marapu eṉappaṭum. tolkāppiyar mutaṟkaṇ eḻuttatikārattaiyē iyaṟṟiṉā reṉṟum, ataṉāl  nūl eṉpatu eḻuttatikārattaiyē kuṟikkumeṉṟum, oru karuttait terivittirukkiṉṟēṉ. nūṉmarapu tamiḻ olikaḷaiyum  avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭṭaiyum meymmayakkaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟu kiṉṟatu. avai yāvum eḻuttaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇṭuḷḷa viḷakkaṅ kaḷum iyalvaraiyaṟaikaḷum perumpālum akkālattu vaḻakkilirunta neṭuṅkaṇakkolikaḷiṉ varicai muṟaiyait taḻuvi yuḷḷaṉa. akarattil toṭaṅki ṉakarattil muṭiyum mutaleḻuttukaḷ muppatu; avai iyaṟkai yāka neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. mīṇṭum avar (neṭuṅ kaṇakkil) auvoṭu muṭiyum paṉṉīrolikaḷai uyir eṉṟum, avaṟ ṟukkup piṉ vantu ṉavvoṭu muṭiyum patiṉeṭṭai mey eṉṟum kūṟu kiṉṟār. ataṉpiṉ vevvēṟolikaḷiṉ aḷapu toṭarkiṉṟatu. av vaṭippaṭaiyil, oru māttiraiyuḷḷa uyir kuṟil eṉṟum, irumāttirai yuḷḷa uyir neṭil eṉṟum pākupāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu. mey yeḻuttiṉ māttirai arai.
maṟuppu
vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttukaḷ mottam 48 eṉṟum, 52 eṉṟum, 53 eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyariṭaik karuttuvēṟupāṭiruntu varuvatu pōṉṟu, tamiḻilum cārpeḻuttuppaṟṟik karuttuvēṟupāṭu toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu vantirukkiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉṟār. atu muṉṉūlkaḷait taḻuviyatu. pavaṇantiyār cārpeḻuttup patteṉṟār. iv viruvarkkum iṭaippaṭṭa amitacākaraṉār, cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟun taṉmaiya veṉṟā eṉṟu tokai kuṟiyātu kūṟiṉum, ataṉ uraiyāciriyar,

iṉi, tolkāppiyar kālattil nūl eṉṟatu potuvakaippaṭṭa pottakattaiyaṉṟu; ētēṉum oruvakai aṟiviyalaiyē  (Science). iyal, icai, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻum taṉittaṉi ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ilakkaṇamellām aṟiviyal pōṉṟiruppatāl nūl eṉappaṭum. maruttuvam, kaṇitam, kaṇiyam (vāṉanūl) eṉpaṉavum avai pōṉṟa piṟavum aṟiviyalkaḷē yātalāl, avaiyum nūleṉappaṭum.

yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar	(cey. 78)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvaiyum ataṉ uraiyaiyum nōkkuka.
ilakkaṇam oru moḻipaṟṟiya aṟiviyalā yiruttalāl nūl eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉaik kūṟum pottakamum nūl eṉavēpaṭum. e-ṭu: mativāṇaṉār nāṭakat tamiḻnūl.
ākavē, nūṉmarapu eṉpatu, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil marapākak koḷḷappaṭṭuvarum eḻuttukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭum eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumē yaṉṟi, nūṉmarapu oṉṟē tolkāppiyar mutaṉmutal eḻutiya ilakkaṇanūl eṉṟu poruḷkoḷḷa iṭantarātu.
eḻuttatikāram nūṉmarapu, moḻimarapu, piṟappiyal, puṇariyal, tokaimarapu, urupiyal, uyirmayaṅkiyal, puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉat toṇ (oṉpāṉ) pakutippaṭṭiruppatum, nūṉmarapu eṉpatu eḻuttatikāram muḻuvataiyum kuṟikkaviyalāteṉpatait teṟṟeṉat terivikkum.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻutti ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, ataiyē mutal tolkāppiyar nūlāka vaṭittār eṉṟum, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām avarālum iraṇṭām tolkāppiyarālum pintic ceyyappaṭṭavaiyeṉṟum, iru tavaṟāṉa aṭippaṭaiyilēyē par. te.po.mī.yiṉ ārāycci iyaṅku vatāl, mutaṟkōṇal muṟṟuṅ kōṇalā yiruppatai neṭukaluṅ kāṇalām.

eṉṉum  paṉampāraṉār potuppāyirattoṭu par. te.po.mī.-yiṉ koḷkai muṟṟum muraṇpaṭutal kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
puḷḷi avar (tolkāppiyar) cila eḻuttukaḷiṉ varivaṭivaik kūṟap pukumiṭattup puḷḷikkoḷkaiyaip pukuttukiṉṟār. vēṟō riṭattil viḷakkiyuḷḷavāṟu, avar puḷḷiyuṭaiya meyyeḻuttukaḷoṭu toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu viḷaṅkakkūṭiyatu. ekara okara uyirk kuṟilkaḷum puḷḷi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. itu uyirmey eṉṉum acai yeḻuttaippaṟṟik kūṟa vaḻikāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṟkiṭaiyē mikak kuṟukiya makaramākiya makarakkuṟukkattiṉ vaṇṇaṉai varukiṉṟatu. ataṉ varivaṭivam puḷḷikku allatu meyyeḻuttiṟku muṉpē kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. iṉṉō roḻuṅkiṉmaiyum uḷḷatu. nūṉmarapu eḻuttoli kaḷait taṉitta vaṭiviṟ kūṟukiṉṟatu. moḻimarapu avaṟṟiṉ puṇariyal vēṟupāṭukaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟatu. iv vēṟupāṭṭiṟku māṟāka, oru puṇariyal vēṟupāṭākiya makarak kuṟukkam nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. teḷivāka, itu ōr iṭaiccerukal. mayilainātar makarakkuṟukkattiṟku mēṟkōḷ vēṟu nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu kāṭṭit tolkāppiyattiṉiṉṟu kāṭṭāmai, ciṟappākak kavaṉikkat takkatu. makarakkuṟukkam paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpā piṟkālat tolkāppiyattaic cērntatāyirukkalāmā?
maṟuppu
puḷḷiyeṉpatu tolkāppiyar putitāka vakuttataṉṟu. uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakai yeḻuttum mutaṉmutalākak koṇṭa tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku, varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta kumarināṭṭu viṉaiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu. meyyeḻuttiṟkuk kuṟiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu puḷḷi. itu piṉṉar virivāka viḷakkappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar nūṉmarapil eḻuttukaṭku aḷavu kūṟivarumiṭattu,
meyyi ṉaḷapē yaraiyeṉa moḻipa 	(11)
eṉṟu meyyeḻuttiṟku aḷapu kuṟittapiṉ, cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟum avvaḷapē koḷvatāl,
avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē 	(12)
eṉṟu nūṟpāviyaṟṟi, araiyiṉuṅ kuṟukik kālāka volikkum makarak kuṟukkattai,
``araiyaḷavu kuṟukal makara muṭaittē
icaiyiṭaṉ arukun teriyuṅ kālai	(13)
eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṉ ciṟappāṉa varivaṭivattai,
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē	(14)
eṉṟu aṟivittār.
iṅṅaṉam, uriya iṭattilum toṭarcciyāṉa muṟaiyilum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷa puḷḷic ceytiyai,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal	(15)
eṉṉum puḷḷiyeḻuttup paṟṟiya nūṟpāvukku muṟpaṭak kūṟiyatu, iṭavaḻu eṉṉum kuṟṟameṉak koḷvatu poruntātu.
eḻutteṉap paṭupa.

muppaḵteṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē.	(1)
avaitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa		(2)
eṉṟu nūl toṭakkattilēyē puḷḷic ceyti kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl, atai āciriyaṉvāyk kēṭṭaṟinta māṇavaṉukku, uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē. eṉpatu putiyatākavum viḷaṅkātatākavum, muṟpaṭak kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatākavum, koḷḷappaṭā teṉka.
iṉi, tokuttuc cuṭṭal, vakuttuk kāṭṭal, virittuk kūṟal, oppiṉ muṭittal, oṉṟiṉam muṭittal, taṉṉiṉa muṭittal, moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉaiyum muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal mutaliya uttikaḷai nōkkiṉum, uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē eṉṉum nūṟpā nūṉmarapil iṭam peṟṟiruppatu vaḻuvākat tōṉṟātu.
mayilainātar, naṉṉūliṉ cārpeḻuttut tokai nūṟpāvukku,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa........mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
	eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum ivvāṟu mutal cārpeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭā reṉka. aḵtē, avar cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟē koṇṭā rālō veṉiṉ, aḵtē, naṉṟu coṉṉāy! oḻintavai eppāṟpaṭu meṉṟārkku mūṉṟāvatoru pakuti collalāva tiṉmai yāṉum, mutaleḻuttān taṉmai avaṟṟiṟkiṉmaiyāṉum, cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟuta lāṉum, ip pattum cārpākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatu. aḵtē amaika. oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum aikārak kuṟukkamum yāṇṭup aukārak kuṟukkamum makarak kuṟukkamum āytak kuṟukkamum peṟṟāmō veṉiṉ,
vaṉmaiyoṭu raḵkāṉ ḻaḵkā ṉoḻintāṅ
kaṉmey āytamō ṭaḷapeḻum orōvaḻi.
aḷapeṭai taṉiyiraṇ ṭalvaḻi aiau
uḷatām oṉṟarai taṉimaiyu mākum.
patiṉeṇ meyyum atuvē mavvō
ṭāytamum aḷaparai tēytalum urittē
eṉṟār āciriyar avinayaṉārum eṉak koḷka eṉa vuraittuḷḷār.
itil, avinaya nūṟpāvē pōtumātaliṉ, tolkāppiya  nūṟpā eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭavillai. ataṉāl, tolkāppiya makarak kuṟukka nūṟpāvaippaṟṟi nām aiyuṟa vēṇṭiyatillai. ikarak kuṟukkam, ukarak kuṟukkam, aikārak kuṟukkam, aukārak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam mutaliyaṉa vum tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, avaṟṟaiyellām mayilainātar mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭavillai, aṅṅaṉamē makarak kuṟukkamum eṉka.
iṉit tolkāppiya makarak kuṟukka nūṟpā piṟkālattatāyiṉ,
ceyyuḷ iṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum. 		(moḻi. 18)
ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum 		(moḻi. 19)
eṉpaṉavum piṟkālattaṉavēyātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam ākāmai kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
oliyaṉkaḷiṉ pātīṭu
pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷum oliyaṉkaḷiṉ pātīṭṭai virittuk kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. naṉṉūl atai mutalīṟiṭainilai yeṉkiṉṟatu. tol kāppiyar, eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, mutalilumiṟutiyilumvarumoliyaṉkaḷaimoḻimarapilum,meymmayakkaṅkaḷainūṉmarapilum,eṭuttukkūṟukiṉṟār.....im meymmayakkam  taṉic collilum toṭarc collilum kūṭṭuc collilum nikaḻum. eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉic collil vaḻaṅkum meymmayakkaṅkaḷaiyē eṭuttuk kūṟi, eṭuttuk kāṭṭillāta meymmayakkaṅkaḷellām iṟantupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟoru koḷkaiyai mēṟkoḷkiṉṟār. uṇmaiyil nūṉmarapiṟ kovvāta cuṭṭuviṉā aṭikaḷum atiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟuppu
āriya iṉam tōṉṟu muṉpē muttamiḻilakkaṇam kumarināṭṭil muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntataṉālum, mullai nākarika nilaiyiṉarāṉa āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālattil vaṭaintiyāvil tamiḻarum vatintiruntataṉālum, irāmāyaṇak kālattilēyē akattiyar teṉṉāṭu vantu eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻ iyampi māpiṇṭamākiya akattiyam iyaṟṟiyataṉālum, āriya mutalilakkiyamākiya irukku vētam intiyā vilēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭataṉālum, pala pirāticākkiya nūṟpākkaḷ tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷai ottiruppataṉālum, pāṇiṉīyattukku muntiya mutaṟ camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇamākiya aintiram tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟi maṟaintataṉālum, pirāticākkiyaṅkaṭ kellām tamiḻilakkaṇamē mūlam eṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malaiyām.
uṭaṉilai meymmayakkam, vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkam eṉṉum iruvakai meymmayakkaṅkaḷuḷ, piṉṉataip paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉpaṭi, pala meymmayakkaṅkaḷ tokaiccol eṉṉum kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷilum toṭarc coṟkaḷilumē nikaḻum.
e-kā :	ḷca - nīḷciṉai,	lya - kolyāṉai,		
	ḷya - veḷyāṟu,	ṇca - veṇcāntu,	
	ṇña - veṇñāṇ,	ṇya - maṇyāṟu,
	ṇva - eṇvaṭṭu,	ṉca - puṉcey,	
	ṉña - meṉñāṇ,	ṉya - iṉyāḻ,
	ṉva - puṉvaraku,	mva - varum vaṇṇakkaṉ,
	yca - eycilai,	rca - vārcilai,
	ḻca - vāḻcēri,	yña - pāyñekiḻi,
	rña - nērñekiḻi,	ḻña - vāḻñeṇṭu,
	rya - pōryāṉai,	ḻya - vīḻyāṉai,
	yṅa - vēyṅṅaṉam,	rṅa - vērṅṅaṉam,	
	ḻṅa - vēḻṅṅaṉam.
ñya, nya, mya, vya, eṉpavaṟṟiṟku uraiyāciriyar kāṭṭiya uriñyātu, porunyātu, tirumyātu, tevyātu eṉpavaṟṟai  uriñ yāṉai, porunyāṟu, tirumyāmai, tevyāṭu eṉa māṟṟiṉ, nacciṉark kiṉiyar kūṟum kuṟṟam nīṅkiviṭum.
ellāt tokaiyum orucol naṭaiya	(ecca. 24)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamē kūṟuvatāl, meymmayakkattukkut taṉiccoṟ pōṉṟē kūṭṭuc collum  koḷḷappaṭum.
iṉṟu taṉiccol eṭuttukkāṭṭillā meymmayakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟukku, muṉkālattil taṉiccolliruntu piṉṉar iṟantupaṭ ṭirukkalām. āyiṉ, taṉiccollē eṭuttukkāṭṭukkut takum eṉa varaiyaṟuppatu poruntātu.
orē col allatu eḻuttamaiti vevvēṟu vakaiyil vevvēṟu iyalukku uriyatākalām. amma eṉṉum viḷippeyar kēṭpikkum iṭaiccollāyiṉ iṭaiyiyalukkuriyatu. karuvi eṉṉum tokutip peyar ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuppaṟṟi uriyiyalukkuriyatu. iṅṅaṉamē cila meym mayakkaṅkaḷum eḻuttiyalukkum puṇariyalukkum uriyaṉavākum. ātalāl, kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻip puṇarcciyākaliṉ īṇṭaikkākā eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vilakkuvatu poruntātu.
iṉi, nya, ṇya, mya, ṉya eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa meymmayakkaṅkaḷ tamiḻil taṉiccoṟkup poruntāvātaliṉ, eṉṟum kūṭṭuc colli lēyē nikaḻum. avaṟṟait taṉiccoṟkum uriyaṉaveṉṟu kūṟi, tamiḻai vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamākkavum vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyateṉak kāṭṭavum muyalvar, akappakaiyum puṟappakaiyumākiya tamiḻppakaivar cilar.
cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷum viṉāveḻuttukaḷum tamiḻ marapeḻuttu kaḷātaliṉ, avaiyum tolkāppiya nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu mayaṅkiyō mayaṅkātō kūṟuvataṉālum, ic cuṭṭeḻuttum viṉāveḻuttum vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmaiyāla vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇattil kūṟappaṭāmaiyālum, iv viruvakai yeḻuttukaḷum nūṉ marapiṟkup poruntāveṉap par. te. po. mī. kūṟuvatu avar piṟaḻ vuṇarcciyaiyē piṟaṅkak kāṭṭum. 
kūṟṟu
moḻimarapu
aṭutta iyal moḻimarapu. itil avar i,u, āytam  eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ kuṟukkaṅkaḷākiya puṇariyal vēṟupāṭukaḷaik kūṟukiṟār. am mūṉṟum taṉiccoṟkaḷilum puṇarccoṟkaḷilum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. camaṟkirutap pulutam (pluta) eṉṉum uyiraḷapeṭaiyai aṟintukoṇṭu, irumāttiraikku mēlolikkum neṭṭuyir tamiḻil illai yeṉṟu ēṟkeṉavē vācakarai  eccarittirukkiṉṟār. āyiṉ, āṟām nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyuḷḷataiyē ēṉ iṅkut tiruppik kūṟavēṇṭu meṉpatu teḷivā yillai. orukāl, aḷapeṭai uyirneṭiliṉ, puṇariyal vēṟupāṭāka nikaḻkiṉṟateṉṟu iṅkuk kūṟukiṉṟār pōlum!
maṟuppu
tolkāppiyam iṭaikkaḻakak kālattil iyaṉṟa palvēṟu taṉittamiḻ muṉṉūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu tokutta tokaic cārpu nūlātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻip puluta aṟivaikkoṇṭu.
mūvaḷa picaittal ōreḻut tiṉṟē	(eḻuttu. 5)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, kūṟṟuppōṟ koṭiyatē.
eḻutteṉappaṭupa.......muppaḵteṉpa.”	(eḻuttu. 1)
a i  u eo.......kuṟṟeḻut teṉpa.	(eḻuttu. 3)
ā ī  ū ē  ai ōōḷa.....neṭṭeḻut teṉpa.	(eḻuttu. 4)nīṭṭamvēṇṭiṉ....... eṉmaṉār pulavar. 	(eḻuttu. 6)
eṉṟu palarpālilēyē tamiḻamuṉṉūlārait tolkāppiyar kuṟit tiruttal kāṇka.
īraḷapicaittalōreḻuttiṉṟē”eṉṟuvaraiyaṟuttataṉāl,ataṟkuvilakkākavarumciṟupāṉmanikaḻcciyāṉaaḷapeṭaiyamāṇavaṉaṟitaṟporuṭṭu,ataṉaiyaṭuttu,nīṭṭamvēṇṭiṉ..... eṉmaṉār pulavar, eṉṟu potuppaṭac curukkik kūṟiṉār. āyiṉ, uyiraḷa peṭai eṉṟu evvāṟu  nikaḻumeṉṟum, ataṟku aṭaiyāḷam etuveṉ ṟum, ettaṉai māttiraiyeṉṟum, virivākat teriyavēṇṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟai iṅku moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. eḻuttiṉ taṉinilai paṟṟiyatu eḻuttiyal; ataṉ coṉṉilai paṟṟiyatu moḻiyiyal.
ōreḻuttuc collāyiṉ, taṉinilaiyilum iruvakai vaḻakkilum aḷapeṭai nikaḻum.
e-kā : 	ēeee aṇṇā!-ulakiyalaḷapeṭai
	ōo iṉitē (kuṟaḷ. 1176) - ceyyuḷiyalaḷapeṭai
kūṟṟu
oliyiyaṟ collaip piṉṉar āyattoṭaṅki ōreḻuttuc col, īreḻuttuc col, mūveḻuttuc col eṉa atai mūvakaiyāka vakukkiṉṟār. ataṉpiṉ mutaleḻuttukaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟār. makarak kuṟukkam mīṇṭum orumuṟai collappaṭukiṉṟatu. aikārak kuṟukkamum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mūṉṟiṟku  mēṟpaṭṭa puṇariyal eḻuttu vēṟupāṭu kaḷai avar oppukkoḷḷāviṭiṉ, ik kuṟukkaṅkaḷai ivviṭattiṟ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. piṟkālattār aikāra aukāraṅkaḷai neṭilkaḷākac ceyyuḷil āḷvataṉ iṭarppāṭṭai uṇarnta pōtu, pōlik koḷkaiyaip pukuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
makarakkuṟukkattai moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiyatu, kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamaṉṟu. nūṉmarapil eḻuttukaḷiṉ māttirai yaḷavaik kūṟumiṭattu, makarakkuṟukka māttirai kāleṉṟu kūṟa nērntatu. moḻimarapil makarak kuṟukkam nikaḻum iṭattaic colla vēṇṭiyiruntataṉāl,


ceyyu ḷiṟutip  pōli  moḻivayiṉ
ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum 			(18)
ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum 			(19)
eṉṟār āciriyar. īriṭattum kūṟavēṇṭiyatu kūṟalēyaṉṟik kūṟiyatu kūṟalaṉṟu. 
araiyaḷapu kuṟukal makara muṭaittē
icaiyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅ kālai 	(nūṉ. 13)
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē	(nūṉ. 14)
eṉṉum iv viru nūṟpākkaḷaiyum, mēṟkūṟiya moḻimarapu nūṟpākka ḷuṭaṉ oppunōkki uṇmai kāṇka.
tolkāppiyattiṉ muṉṉūlkaḷāṉa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷellām, iyalpāṉa tamiḻeḻuttukaḷai mottam muppateṉṟē koṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ nāṉkiṉ iṭavēṟupāṭṭālum puṇarcci viḷaivālum ēṟpaṭṭa mut tiripolikaḷaic cārpeḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, iyalpāṉa mūlamuppatai yum mutaleḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, vēṟupaṭutti vaḻaṅkiṉum, mutaleḻuttu kaḷaiyē mutaṉmaiyākak karutiṉaveṉpatu,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya mutal nūṟpāvāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
ellā meyyoṭuṅ kūṭā īruyirum uyirēṟā oru meyyumāṉa muccārpeḻuttukaḷuḷ iraṇṭu caṟṟuk kuṟukiyolippiṉum, olittiripē cārpeḻuttukaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpām. piṟkālap patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa naṉṉūlār, olik kuṟukkamum nīṭṭamum kalappumāṉa iyalpukaḷaiyuñ cērttu, cārpeḻuttup patteṉa virittār. ik          koḷkai tolkāppiyarukkillai. āyiṉ, olittiripu pōṉṟē olikkuṟukka nīṭṭamum coṉṉilaik kuriyavātaliṉ, avaṟṟai moḻi marapiṟ kūṟiṉār. ātalāl, makarak kuṟukkamum aikārak kuṟukkamum kūṟuvataṟku moḻimarapē takka iyalām.

eṉpataṉāl,
aiviyap pākum 	(868)
eṉṉumiṭattum, aikāram irumāttirai neṭilēyātal vēṇṭum.

aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa	(1091)
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, aiyar eṉṉum vaṭivamē toṉṟutoṭṭu mūlattilum uraiyilum vaḻaṅkivarutal kāṇka.
kūṟṟu
yakarattiṉ maṭṭiṭṭa vaḻakku vēṟōriṭattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cakaramey akarattoṭum aikāra aukāraṅkaḷōṭum kūṭi varuvatillai. akarattoṭu varāmaikkuk  kāraṇam, aṇṇameyyākiya cakarattiṉ piṉvarum akaram aṇṇavuyirākiya ekaramākat tirintuviṭuvatē. itu tolkāppiyar kāla moḻinilaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. an nilai pala cakara mutaṟcoṟkaḷ vantuḷḷa kaḻaka ilakkiyak kāla nilaiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟu paṭṭatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
maṟuppu
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟ cakara mutaṟcol oṉṟēṉum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkavillaiyeṉpatu caṟṟum poruntātu. cakkaram, cakaṭam mutaliya orucila (vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyākiya) irumoḻip potuc coṟkaḷai nīkkiṉum, peruvāri ulaka vaḻakkum iṉṟiyamaiyā aṭippaṭaiyumāṉa cakku, cakkai, cakati, caṅku, caccaravu, caṭṭam, caṭṭi, caṭṭu, caṭṭai, caṭaṅku, caṭār, caṭai, caṭaivu, caṇṇu, caṇpu, caṇṭi, caṇṭu, caṇṭai, caṇāy, catakku, catuppu, catai, cantu, cantai, cappaṭṭai, cappaḷi, cappāṇi, cappu, cappai, campaḷam, campā, cammaṇam, camaṭṭi, camam, camaḻ, camai, car, caracara, caraṭṭu, caraḷ, cari, caruku, caruvu, carēl, calli, calakku, calacalappu, calaṅkai, calavai, cali, cavvu, cavaṭṭu, cavar, cavaḷi, cavai, caḻakku, caḻi, caḷ, caḷḷai, caḷappu, caḷār, caḷi, caṟṟu, caṟāmpu, caṟukku mutaliya ērāḷamāṉa cakaramutaṟ coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkup piṉṉarē tamiḻil tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatu, eḷḷaḷavum iyaṟkaikku ēṟkātu. āriyar vanta kālattiṉiṉṟu āṅkilar vanta kālamvarai, tamiḻukkup paṭippaṭiyāka mēṉmēlum kēṭu nērntatēyaṉṟi ākkam nēravillai. tammaic cūttirar eṉṟum, tam moḻiyai iḻintār moḻiyeṉṟum tāmē tāḻttikkoṇṭu, āriyarai nilattēvareṉṟum avaratu ilakkiyamoḻiyait tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum nampi, iyaṉṟavarai tamiḻc collai vaḻakku vīḻttic camaṟkirutac collaiyē makiḻcciyōṭum perumitattōṭum vaḻaṅkivanta aṭimait tamiḻar, eṅṅaṉam putut taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷaip puṉaintiruttal kūṭum?
ākavē, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālac cakaramutal tamiḻccoṟka ḷellām āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅkālattaṉavē, itai,

vantataṉāṟ cammutalum vai
eṉṉum mayilainātarurai mēṟkōḷālum teḷika.
